HandMvNet also achieves a substantial reduction in inference time while delivering competitive results compared to the state-of-the-art methods, making it suitable for real-time applications. Evaluated on publicly available datasets, HandMvNet qualitatively and quantitatively outperforms previous methods under identical settings. Code is available at github.com/pyxploiter/handmvnet.

</details>


### [109] [Structured Affinity for Unsupervised Visual Class-Incremental Memory in Deep Artificial Immune Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20104)
*Siphesihle Sithungu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化、无梯度免疫亲和力的深度人工免疫网络（Deep AIN），将其作为无需回放（replay-free）的视觉类增量表示记忆学习器。通过在多个数据集（sklearn digits、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、KMNIST）上的实验，证明了特征图级绑定简介（binding-profile）保留、自适应潜在坐标重组和层间尺度校准是关键机制，下游探针达到最高0.978的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工免疫网络（AINs）虽天然具有记忆形成特性，但现有的视觉AIN通常使用扁平化向量亲和度，忽略了空间结构信息。这限制了AIN在视觉类增量学习中作为无需回放的表示记忆系统的潜力，因此需要研究结构化、无梯度的免疫亲和机制。

Method: 将视觉B细胞形式化为结构化模板，包括移动模板亲和度、零归一化互相关（ZNCC）滤波器以及特征图绑定简介。将抗体库既视为记忆又视为表示归纳基；通过将绑定简介响应图传入后续免疫层获得深度。引入自适应潜在坐标重组和层间尺度校准机制，无需回放、无需标签驱动的免疫更新、也无需通过免疫层反向传播。

Result: 在sklearn digits上，下游探针在全部十个类别出现后，逻辑回归达到0.939最终平衡准确率，1-近邻达到0.902，初始类别保留率为0.978。自适应层间尺度校准将两层特征图Deep AIN提升至0.978平衡准确率。Fashion-MNIST达到0.814，KMNIST达到0.853。

Conclusion: 结构化亲和度、响应图保留、自适应潜在重组和层间尺度校准是无需回放的视觉免疫记忆的关键机制。特征图级Deep AIN能够在无回放、无反向传播条件下学习类判别性视觉记忆，为视觉类增量学习提供了新的免疫记忆范式。

Abstract: Artificial immune networks (AINs) are naturally memory-forming systems, but conventional visual AINs often rely on flattened vector affinity that ignores spatial structure. This paper studies whether structured, gradient-free immune affinity can make Deep AINs viable as replay-free visual class-incremental representation-memory learners. Visual B-cells are formalized as structured templates, including shifted-template affinity, zero-normalized cross-correlation (ZNCC) filters, and feature-map binding profiles. A repertoire is treated both as memory and as a representation-inducing basis, while depth is obtained by passing binding-profile response maps to subsequent immune layers. The resulting Deep AIN exhibits adaptive latent coordinate reorganization: as new classes arrive, the binding-profile space evolves while retaining recoverable structure for earlier classes. Experiments on sklearn digits, MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and KMNIST show that preserving response maps is critical. Scalar binding-profile variants underperform, whereas feature-map Deep AINs learn class-discriminative visual memory without replay, label-driven immune updates, or backpropagation through the immune layers. On sklearn digits, downstream probes fitted on the learned binding profiles reach 0.939 final balanced accuracy with logistic regression and 0.902 with 1-nearest-neighbour after all ten classes are encountered, with initial-class retention of 0.978. Adaptive layer-wise scale calibration further improves the two-layer feature-map Deep AIN to 0.978 balanced accuracy. With the same calibration rule, Fashion-MNIST reaches 0.814 and KMNIST reaches 0.853. These probes are external validation tools, not components of the AIN. The results identify structured affinity, response-map preservation, adaptive latent reorganization, and layer-wise scale calibration as key mechanisms for replay-free visual immune memory.

</details>


### [110] [BeyondMasks: Evaluating Causal and Physical Consistency in Video Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20107)
*Yigit Ekin,Enes Sanli,Aykut Erdem,Erkut Erdem,Aysegul Dundar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文引入BeyondMasks基准，一个用于因果一致视频目标移除的配对数据集，包含时间对齐的合成与真实世界视频对及干净背景参考；并提出CORE评估协议，基于结构化视觉语言模型联合衡量目标消失与后续效应一致性，揭示现有SOTA方法在去除次级物理效应（阴影、反射等）上的系统性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标移除评估仅关注掩码区域保真度，将其视为局部图像修复问题。然而真实场景中目标移除是一种因果干预，消除目标还需移除其诱导的物理效应（阴影、反射、光照变化、半透明、动态痕迹）。现有基准缺乏对齐的干净参考或局限于简化的合成场景，无法系统评估因果一致性。

Method: 构建BeyondMasks配对基准，包含时间对齐的合成与真实视频对以及干净背景参考，覆盖多样的光度、几何、体积和动态交互，支持基于掩码和指令驱动的编辑；提出CORE评估协议，一种基于结构化视觉语言模型的评估方法，联合测量目标消失程度与后效应一致性，并与人类判断对齐。

Result: 对SOTA方法的基准测试揭示了在掩码区域保真度较高的情况下，方法在移除次级物理效应方面存在系统性失败，暴露了视觉真实性与因果正确性之间的差距。

Conclusion: BeyondMasks将视频目标移除重新定义为因果场景一致性而非局部重建，并提供了统一的评估框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models have significantly improved visual realism in video object removal, yet evaluation protocols still focus on masked region fidelity, treating removal as local inpainting. In real scenes, object removal is a causal intervention: eliminating an object also requires removing its induced physical effects, such as shadows, reflections, illumination changes, translucency, and dynamic traces. Existing benchmarks lack aligned clean references or remain limited to simplified synthetic settings, preventing systematic evaluation of causal consistency. We introduce BeyondMasks, a paired benchmark for causally consistent video object removal, consisting of temporally aligned synthetic and real world video pairs with clean background references. The dataset spans diverse photometric, geometric, volumetric, and dynamic interactions, and supports both mask based and instruction driven editing. We further propose CORE, a structured vision language model based evaluation protocol that jointly measures object disappearance and after effect consistency, aligning more closely with human judgments than existing metrics. Benchmarking state of the art methods reveals systematic failures in removing secondary physical effects despite high masked region fidelity, exposing a gap between visual plausibility and causal correctness. BeyondMasks reframes video object removal as causal scene consistency rather than local reconstruction and provides a unified framework for its evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [ArmorOCR: Grounded Adversarial Visual Perception via Observation-Transferred Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20122)
*Linhan Cao,Siyuan Li,Jun Lan,Liangbo He,Guannan Li,Xiaolei Huang,Jun Jia,Shuheng Zhou,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvSpot，首个面向对抗性OCR的grounded感知评估基准，包含390张带区域级标注的图像，覆盖5大类13种细粒度对抗性OCR类型；并提出ArmorOCR两阶段训练框架，通过On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)获取缺失的对抗性OCR感知能力，再通过Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)配合任务条件化奖励细化grounded感知，在对抗性与通用OCR任务上均取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言多模态模型虽具备强OCR识别能力，但对人类可读但模型难以定位和识别的对抗性视觉文本仍较脆弱。现有OCR基准主要聚焦自然或文档风格文本，对抗性OCR评估在规模、任务覆盖或区域感知评估方面存在局限，缺乏系统性的grounded对抗性OCR基准。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架ArmorOCR：(1) 通过On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)从特权变换观测中获取缺失的对抗性OCR感知能力；(2) 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)结合任务条件化奖励（定位、识别、完整spotting及VQA）来细化grounded OCR感知。同时构建AdvSpot基准，包含390张带区域标注的图像，覆盖5大类别13种细粒度对抗性OCR类型。

Result: 在AdvSpot、其他对抗性OCR基准以及通用OCR基准上的实验表明，ArmorOCR在一致性地提升对抗性OCR感知能力的同时，保持了有竞争力的通用OCR性能。

Conclusion: ArmorOCR有效解决了对抗性OCR感知挑战，验证了两阶段训练框架在同时兼顾对抗场景鲁棒性与通用OCR能力保持方面的有效性，为后续对抗性grounded OCR研究提供了基准与训练范式。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have demonstrated strong OCR recognition capabilities, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial visual text that is readable to humans but challenging for models to localize and recognize. Existing OCR benchmarks mainly focus on natural or document-style text, while adversarial OCR evaluations remain limited in scale, task coverage, or region-aware evaluation. In this paper, we formulate adversarial OCR as a \textbf{grounded OCR perception} task and introduce \textbf{AdvSpot}, the first benchmark for grounded adversarial OCR evaluation. AdvSpot comprises 390 images with region-level annotations, spanning 5 primary categories and 13 fine-grained adversarial OCR types. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{ArmorOCR}, a two-stage training framework for robust adversarial OCR perception. ArmorOCR first acquires missing adversarial OCR perception from privileged transformed observations through On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), and then refines grounded OCR perception through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with task-conditioned rewards for localization, recognition, full spotting, and visual question answering (VQA). Experiments on our AdvSpot, other adversarial OCR benchmarks, and general OCR benchmarks demonstrate that ArmorOCR consistently improves adversarial OCR perception while preserving competitive general OCR capability.

</details>


### [112] [ID-VTG: Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20127)
*Minghang Zheng,Jingli Wei,Hongyi Yang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像消歧视频时序定位任务（ID-VTG），利用"参考图像+文本描述"的多模态查询来精确定位特定实例执行特定动作的视频片段，并构建了两个基准数据集（IDVTG-Gym和IDVTG-InternVid），提出基于双分支快慢架构的VGD-Agg框架，通过Compare Token和Depress Value提升区分能力，达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频时序定位（VTG）在处理涉及视觉上相似的多个实体时存在困难，因为仅依赖自然语言查询难以准确描述细粒度的视觉属性。当需要区分多个外观相似的事件时，文本描述往往不够精确，这限制了定位的准确性。

Method: 提出ID-VTG任务，采用参考图像+文本描述的组合查询；构建IDVTG-Gym（体操精细动作）和IDVTG-InternVid（开放世界）两个基准；提出VGD-Agg框架，基于双分支快慢架构——快速分支生成初步事件候选，慢速分支进行视频帧与参考图像的细粒度匹配；引入两个可学习的token：Compare Token（表示难负样本，探测目标实例是否存在）和Depress Value（表示与文本无关的事件），通过将缺乏目标实例的候选推向Depress Value来简化文本消歧过程。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性，取得了最先进（SOTA）的结果。

Conclusion: 引入图像作为额外查询模态可以有效解决视频时序定位中视觉相似实体难以通过纯文本区分的问题，双分支架构配合可学习token的对比/抑制机制能够显著提升定位精度。

Abstract: Video Temporal Grounding (VTG) faces significant challenges when natural language queries must distinguish between multiple events involving visually similar entities, particularly when relying on fine-grained visual attributes that are difficult to describe accurately in words alone. To address this, we introduce Image-Disambiguated Video Temporal Grounding (ID-VTG), a task that leverages multimodal queries combining a reference image and a text description to precisely localize segments where a specific instance performs a described action. To facilitate research, we construct two benchmarks: IDVTG-Gym, focusing on fine-grained, compositionally ordered gymnastics actions with athletes in similar uniforms; and IDVTG-InternVid, an open-world dataset featuring diverse entities (e.g., humans, animals, fictional characters) and significant temporal distractors. Methodologically, we propose the Visually-Guided Disambiguation Aggregation (VGD-Agg) framework based on a dual-branch fast-slow architecture. The fast branch efficiently generates preliminary event proposals, while the slow branch performs fine-grained frame-level matching between video frames and the reference image. We enhance discriminability via two learnable tokens: a Compare Token, which represents hard negatives to probe for the presence of the target instance (as referred to by the query image), and a Depress Value, which represents text-irrelevant events. Proposals that the Compare Token identifies as lacking the target instance are pushed toward the Depress Value, thus easing disambiguation via the text query. Extensive experiments validate our approach, which achieves state-of-the-art results on the proposed benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/ID-VTG.

</details>


### [113] [Feature Evolution and Migration during Vision Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20134)
*Joonas Järve,Halil Ibrahim Aysel,Tarun Khajuria,Meelis Kull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新视角来可视化Vision Transformers（ViTs）中特征的演化过程，通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从CLS-token表示中提取稀疏特征，并比较它们在网络深度（层）与训练时间（epoch）两个维度上的激活模式。研究发现特征迁移（feature migration）主要集中在训练早期，更多向浅层迁移而非深层，且随着特征组织稳定而减少；同时深层比浅层更早且更强地稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从表示级相似度度量来理解ViTs的训练动态，但这些方法无法揭示特征级别的细粒度演化过程。作者希望超越表示级分析，通过稀疏特征层面来观察特征在训练过程中如何出现、迁移和稳定，从而更深入地理解ViTs的学习机制。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）从CLS-token表示中提取候选稀疏特征，然后在网络深度（layer）和训练时间（epoch）构成的二维网格中比较特征的激活轮廓（activation profiles），以此研究特征级动态，并定义特征迁移（feature migration）来描述特征最可检测层在训练过程中的变化。

Result: 实验发现：1）特征迁移集中在训练早期；2）特征更倾向于向浅层迁移而非深层；3）随着特征组织稳定，迁移现象减少；4）深层比浅层更早且更强烈地趋于稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为理解ViTs学习与演化过程的有效工具，为特征级的训练动态分析提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: We present a novel view on feature evolution in Vision Transformers (ViTs) by visualizing the training process over two dimensions -- network depth (layer) and training time (epochs). We employ Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to extract candidate sparse features from CLS-token representations and compare their activation profiles across epoch--layer pairs. This allows us to study feature-level dynamics that are not directly visible from representation-level similarity measures. Furthermore, we demonstrate how this framework of feature evolution allows us to describe feature migration, the change in the layer where a feature is most detectable during training. Our experiments show that migration is concentrated early in training, occurs more often toward earlier layers than toward deeper layers, and declines as feature organization stabilizes. We further find that deeper layers stabilize earlier and more strongly than shallow layers. The results show that our approach can be employed as a tool for understanding how ViTs learn and evolve.

</details>


### [114] [DPC-Net: Dual-Prior Collaborative Network for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20141)
*Zhaokun He,Kangbiao Shi,Axi Niu,Jian Jin,Peng Wu,Wei Dong,Qingsen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新型双先验协作网络（DPC-Net），通过联合利用退化-语义耦合先验和低级视觉先验实现全合一图像复原，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全合一图像复原方法（AiOIR）在退化建模中常忽略图像语义信息，且重建过程中缺乏低级视觉先验，导致结构失真和语义不一致问题。

Method: DPC-Net包含两个关键组件：1）退化感知网络（DAN）借助视觉-语言模型（VLM）监督，将图像语义引入退化模式编码，并通过退化-语义调制模块（DSMM）实现耦合表征传播；2）解码阶段通过知识库提供低级视觉先验，由双先验协作重建模块（DPCR）整合两种先验信息指导退化去除，同时保留结构和语义。

Result: 在多个复原基准上的大量实验表明，DPC-Net在性能上优于最先进的全合一图像复原方法。

Conclusion: 通过将退化-语义耦合先验与低级视觉先验协同利用，DPC-Net有效解决了现有AiOIR方法在结构保真和语义一致性方面的不足，实现了高质量的全合一图像复原。

Abstract: All-in-One Image Restoration (AiOIR) aims to handle diverse degradations within a unified model. However, existing methods often overlook image semantics in degradation modeling and lack low-level visual priors during reconstruction, leading to structural distortions and semantic inconsistencies. To address these issues, we propose a novel Dual-Prior Collaborative Network (DPC-Net), which achieves high-quality restoration by jointly exploiting degradation-semantic coupled priors and low-level visual priors. Specifically, degraded images are fed into a Degradation-Aware Network (DAN) to extract degradation-semantic coupled features. To this end, a Vision-Language Model (VLM) supervises DAN by constraining its features distribution, introducing image semantics into the encoding of degradation patterns. A Degradation-Semantic Modulation Module (DSMM) further translates this guidance into degradation-semantic coupling and propagates coupled representations to the decoder. During decoding, knowledge bases provide low-level visual priors, and the Dual-Prior Collaborative Reconstruction Module (DPCR) integrates dual-prior information to guide degradation removal while preserving structure and semantics, producing high-fidelity restored images. Extensive experiments on multiple restoration benchmarks demonstrate that DPC-Net achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art AiOIR methods.

</details>


### [115] [PelviNeXt: A Modality-Agnostic Hybrid Network for Pelvic Imaging in Women's Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20144)
*Siam Tahsin Bhuiyan,Rashedur Rahman,Sefatul Wasi,Halima Khatun,Ashraful Islam,AKM Mahbubur Rahman,Saadia Binte Alam,M Ashraful Amin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PelviNeXt，一个模态无关的混合架构，同时应用于盆腔超声和X射线影像。作者在应用过程中发现PCOSGen数据集存在严重的重复污染，并通过感知哈希审计、公开去重版本并建立首个完整性审计的评估协议。在盆腔骨折X射线数据集PXR150上超越了此前最先进结果。消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性健康在医学影像研究中长期资源不足，多囊卵巢综合征（PCOS）和盆腔骨折等盆腔病理缺乏公开、注释良好的基准数据集，亟需一个无需任务特定修改即可跨模态应用的统一架构，以缓解数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出PelviNeXt混合架构：结合稠密卷积特征提取器、分层通道-空间注意力（H-CBAM）、多尺度融合模块（MSFM）和talking-heads多头自注意力（TH-MHSA），不加修改地应用于盆腔超声和X射线两种模态。同时通过感知哈希对PCOSGen数据集进行重复污染审计，发布去重版本并建立5折交叉验证的完整性审计评估基线。

Result: 在PCOSGen上建立了首个完整性审计后的评估基线；在PXR150上，PelviNeXt在准确率、召回率、特异性和AUROC上均超过此前最优结果；消融实验确认每个架构组件对两个任务均有贡献。

Conclusion: 单一架构无需任务特定修改即可作为盆腔影像跨模态的可靠基础，在数据稀缺、研究不足的女性健康领域具有重要价值；同时数据集完整性审计对公平评估和模型可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Women's health remains substantially under-resourced in medical imaging research, with pelvic pathologies such as polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS) and pelvic fracture both suffering from a scarcity of public, well-annotated benchmark data despite their clinical importance. We introduce PelviNeXt, a modality-agnostic hybrid architecture combining a dense convolutional feature extractor, hierarchical channel-spatial attention (H-CBAM), a multi-scale fusion module (MSFM), and talking-heads multi-head self-attention (TH-MHSA), applied without modification to both pelvic ultrasound and X-ray inputs. While benchmarking PelviNeXt on PCOSGen, the only gynaecologist-annotated public PCOS ultrasound dataset, we identified extensive exact and near-duplicate contamination within and across the dataset. We audit this contamination via perceptual hashing, publicly release a deduplicated version of the dataset, and establish the first integrity-audited evaluation protocol and baseline for PCOSGen under 5-fold cross-validation. On the only publicly available pelvic fracture X-ray dataset (PXR150), PelviNeXt exceeds previously reported state-of-the-art results across accuracy, recall, specificity, and AUROC. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component contributes to performance on both tasks. Our results demonstrate that a single architecture, applied without task-specific modification, can serve as a reliable foundation for pelvic imaging across modalities in data-scarce, under-researched areas of women's health.

</details>


### [116] [Artificial Intelligence for Workflow Analysis in Colorectal Surgery: A Multicentric, Cross-Procedural Development and Generalization Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20154)
*Pietro Mascagni,Julia Alekseenko,Pooja P Jain,Marta Goglia,Andrea Balla,Ludovica Baldari,Gianfranco Silecchia,Claudio Fiorillo,Vincenzo Tondolo,Salvador Morales-Conde,Luigi Boni,Sergio Alfieri,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出AI-ColoWorkflow，一个用于微创结直肠手术(MIS-CRS)自动工作流分析的深度学习模型，结合微调后的DINOv3视觉transformer和分层多阶段时序卷积网络，在四中心多中心数据上训练，相位识别宏F1达73.01%，优于中心特异性和手术特异性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动视频化手术工作流评估(VBA)耗时费力，限制了其临床实施。虽然ColoWorkflow工具已验证有效，但需要自动化方法来解决人工评估效率低下的问题。

Method: 收集4个中心的MIS-CRS手术视频及公开数据集，按ColoWorkflow标准手动标注手术阶段(steps)和步骤(phases)。构建深度学习模型，采用微调后的DINOv3视觉transformer进行逐帧视觉特征提取，结合分层多阶段时序卷积网络(TCN)，将相位与步骤识别进行联合优化。模型在汇集的多中心数据上训练，并与中心特异性和手术特异性模型在独立测试集上对比，使用宏F1、平衡准确率、精确率和召回率评估。

Result: AI-ColoWorkflow在相位识别上宏F1为73.01%±10.27（平衡准确率73.43%），步骤识别宏F1为39.82%±7.06（平衡准确率38.65%）。全局模型在大多数实验中优于中心和手术特异性模型，仅手术特异性步骤识别除外。泛化分析中相位识别平均F1为48.42%。

Conclusion: AI-ColoWorkflow能可靠识别MIS-CRS的手术相位。在汇集的多中心、多术式数据上训练的单一模型，在相位识别上的泛化能力至少与中心或手术特异性模型相当且通常更优；但手术特异性模型在特定术式类型上仍具优势，提示未来手术AI开发应采用混合训练策略。

Abstract: Minimally invasive colorectal surgeries (MIS-CRS) are characterised by significant variability and inconsistent outcomes. ColoWorkflow, a tool for the video-based assessment (VBA) of MIS-CRS workflow, was recently validated. However, manual VBA is time-consuming, limiting implementation. This study presents AI-ColoWorkflow, a deep learning model for automated surgical workflow analysis across MIS-CRS. Operative videos of MIS-CRS were collected from 4 centres and a publicly available dataset. Phases and steps were manually annotated according to ColoWorkflow. A deep learning model combining a fine-tuned DINOv3 vision transformer for per-frame visual feature extraction with a hierarchical multi-stage temporal convolutional network was jointly optimized for phase and step recognition. The model trained on pooled multicentric data, namely AI-ColoWorkflow was compared against centre-specific and procedure-specific models on a held-out test set. The following metrics were used for evaluation: macro F1 score, balanced accuracy, precision, and recall. AI-ColoWorkflow achieved a macro F1 of 73.01% $\pm$ 10.27 (balanced accuracy 73.43%) for phase recognition and 39.82% $\pm$ 7.06 (balanced accuracy 38.65%) for step recognition. The global model outperformed centre- and procedure-specific models in most experiments except procedure-specific step recognition. In the generalization analysis, mean F1 was 48.42% for phase recognition. AI-ColoWorkflow can reliably recognize MIS-CRS phases. A single model trained on pooled, multicentric, multi-procedural data generalises at least as well as and often better than centre- or procedure-specific models for phase recognition in MIS-CRS, while procedure-specific step models retain advantages for certain procedure types, motivating hybrid training strategies for future surgical AI development.

</details>


### [117] [G3Ego: Gaze-Guided Graphs for Egocentric Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20157)
*Marko Haralović,Akash Ramakrishnan,Estefania Talavera Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G3Ego是一个基于图的自我中心动作理解框架，利用注视（gaze）作为结构线索来识别场景中与动作相关的实体。它从稀疏采样帧中构建动作场景图，通过注视剪枝无关实体，生成高效且可解释的图嵌入用于动作识别和预测，在EGTEA Gaze+和MECCANO数据集上取得与视频方法相当的性能，并避免了昂贵的视频预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心动作理解方法通常依赖大规模视频模型在外部中心（exocentric）数据集上的预训练，但第一人称动作实际上只涉及少数关键的手-物交互。因此需要一种更高效、更具可解释性且不依赖昂贵视频预训练的替代方案。

Method: G3Ego框架的流程为：(1) 从稀疏采样帧出发；(2) 基于视觉-语言描述、grounded目标物和手部线索构建动作场景图；(3) 利用相机佩戴者的注视信息剪枝图中与动作无关的实体；(4) 将图嵌入进行时间聚合，用于动作识别与动作预测任务。与先前将注视作为辅助模态或注意力信号的做法不同，G3Ego将注视直接融入图构建过程。

Result: 在EGTEA Gaze+和MECCANO数据集上的实验表明，G3Ego与基于视频的方法相比取得了具有竞争力的性能，并且在类不平衡评估下持续提升Macro-F1指标，同时避免了依赖计算成本高昂的视频预训练。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，注视引导的图表示方法能够有效实现自我中心动作理解，兼具高效性和可解释性，专注于与动作相关的交互，为不需要大规模视频预训练的第一人称动作理解提供了可行且有效的方案。

Abstract: Egocentric action understanding is often addressed using large video models pretrained on extensive exocentric datasets. However, many first-person actions depend on a small number of hand-object interactions involving only a few relevant entities.
  We propose G3Ego, a graph-based framework for egocentric action understanding that uses gaze as a structural cue to identify action-relevant entities in the scene. From sparsely sampled frames, G3Ego constructs action scene graphs from vision-language descriptions, grounded objects, and hand cues, and then prunes irrelevant entities using the camera wearer's gaze.
  The resulting graph embeddings are temporally aggregated for action recognition and anticipation. Unlike prior work that uses gaze primarily as an auxiliary modality or attention signal, G3Ego incorporates gaze directly into graph construction, producing efficient and interpretable representations focused on action-relevant interactions.
  Experiments on EGTEA Gaze+ and MECCANO show that G3Ego achieves competitive performance compared with video-based approaches and consistently improves Macro-F1 under class-imbalanced evaluation, while avoiding reliance on computationally expensive video pretraining. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of gaze-guided graph representations for egocentric action understanding.

</details>


### [118] [RoMAN-Flow: Taming Autoregressive Normalizing Flows for Offline Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20208)
*Shaoxuan Wang,Guangting Zheng,Rui Huang,Zhipeng Tang,Sha Zhang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMAN-Flow 是一个离线强化学习框架，通过无采样、优势加权的似然目标函数解决自回归归一化流策略在策略优化阶段的采样瓶颈，并通过知识蒸馏将优化后的策略转化为单步动作生成器以降低部署延迟，在模拟和真实机器人操作任务中取得竞争性性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习可利用历史数据改进机器人策略而无需与环境交互，但现有扩散模型和流匹配策略缺乏可处理的似然函数，限制了基于似然的离线RL后训练。虽然自回归归一化流（AR-NFs）兼具表达力强的动作建模和精确似然评估，但其顺序采样过程在策略优化和部署阶段都带来巨大的采样开销，使其在实践中难以应用。

Method: RoMAN-Flow 提出两种关键技术应对采样瓶颈：(1) 在策略优化阶段采用无采样的优势加权似然目标函数，直接为离线数据集中高优势动作分配更高似然，无需从自回归策略中采样；(2) 在部署阶段通过知识蒸馏将优化后的自回归策略压缩为单步动作生成器，实现低延迟动作预测。

Result: 在多个模拟操作基准测试和真实机器人平台上，RoMAN-Flow 在保持竞争性策略性能的同时，显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了自回归归一化流可以在离线强化学习机器人操作任务中实现实用化，兼顾了精确似然评估的优势与低延迟部署的需求，为基于似然的离线RL后训练提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning improves robotic policies using previously collected data without further environment interaction. Yet prevalent diffusion- and flow-matching robot policies lack tractable likelihoods, limiting their use in likelihood-based offline RL post-training. AR-NFs offer both expressive action modeling and exact likelihood evaluation, but their sequential sampling incurs substantial sampling overhead during policy optimization and deployment. We present RoMAN-Flow (Robotic Manipulation with Autoregressive Normalizing Flows), an offline reinforcement learning framework that makes AR-NF policies practical for robotic manipulation by addressing this sampling bottleneck in both stages. During policy optimization, RoMAN-Flow employs a sampling-free, advantage-weighted likelihood objective that assigns higher likelihood to high-advantage actions from the offline dataset without sampling from the autoregressive policy. For efficient deployment, it distills the optimized autoregressive policy into a one-step action generator, enabling low-latency action prediction. Experiments across multiple simulated manipulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate that RoMAN-Flow achieves competitive policy performance while substantially reducing inference latency. Code is available at https://github.com/konnyaku28/RoMAN-Flow.

</details>


### [119] [Unwarping the Lens: A Physics-Grounded Approach to Video Glasses Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20212)
*Radim Spetlik,David Futschik,Radek Danecek,Feitong Tan,Ziqian Bai,Rohit Pandey,Yinda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的眼镜移除框架，通过从商业级生成模型（Nano Banana, Gemini 3 Pro Image）提取高保真合成人脸图像，经三级结构滤波保持身份、表情和姿态，并在训练中引入基于物理的镜头光学仿真以生成多样化配对数据，最终将知识迁移到专用恢复架构JFSnet中，实现38（实为27.68）FPS的高保真眼镜移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺度生成先验在静态图像修复中虽表现出眼镜移除能力，但缺乏结构约束，导致身份、表情和姿态出现显著的"身份漂移"问题，尤其在动态视频序列中更为严重；且眼镜造成复杂的折射畸变和视角相关镜面反射，使底层面部几何被遮挡，传统方法难以实现高保真移除。

Method: 提出转移框架：1) 从商业级生成模型提取高保真合成人脸图像；2) 通过三阶段结构滤波（结构、语义、空间）对合成图像进行正则化，保持身份、表情和姿态；3) 训练时引入基于物理的镜头光学仿真生成多样化配对数据；4) 将生成模型的多视角知识转移到专用恢复架构JFSnet中，该网络融合DINOv2语义特征与卷积解码器进行空间重建，并利用平移等变约束提升时间一致性和高频细节保留。

Result: 在FFHQ精选子集（12,163张图）上达到高保真和结构精度，推理速度27.68 FPS；在CelebV-Text视频序列的感知研究中，该方法在眼部一致性、时间稳定性和整体恢复质量上一致优于扩散和GAN基线。

Conclusion: 通过"大型生成模型知识蒸馏+专用恢复架构"的转移范式，可以有效解决生成先验缺乏结构约束导致的身份漂移问题，在保持高推理速度的同时实现高保真、时间稳定的眼镜移除，为面部属性编辑提供了一种兼顾质量与效率的可行方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity removal of eyeglasses from video is a major challenge in facial attribute editing, as the underlying facial geometry is often obscured by complex refractive distortions and view-dependent specular reflections. While large-scale generative priors have shown promise in eye-glasses removal via static image inpainting, they often lack the structural constraints necessary to maintain identity, expression, and pose, leading to visible "identity drift" in both static images and dynamic sequences. In this paper, we propose a novel transfer framework that addresses the stochastic nature of generative priors. Our pipeline first extracts high-fidelity synthetic face images from a commercial-grade generative model (Nano Banana, Gemini 3 Pro Image), regularizes them via a three-stage structural filtering process to preserve identity, expression, and pose, and finally applies physically-based simulation of lens optics during training to provide diverse, paired data. This process transfers Nano Banana's photo-realistic, multi-view knowledge into a specialized restoration architecture, JFSnet (Joint Feature-Spatial network). JFSnet integrates DINOv2-based semantic features with a convolutional decoder for spatial reconstruction, leveraging translation equivariance constraints to improve temporal consistency and high-frequency detail preservation. Evaluations on the curated Flickr-Faces-HQ (FFHQ) subset (12,163 images) show that our approach achieves high fidelity and structural accuracy, while maintaining inference speed of 27.68 FPS. In perceptual studies on CelebV-Text video sequences, our results are consistently preferred over diffusion and GAN-based baselines for ocular consistency, temporal stability, and overall restoration quality.

</details>


### [120] [Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention for Hierarchical Feature Modulation toward Anatomy-Agnostic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20229)
*Mosharof Hossain,Md Rabiul Islam,Limon Halder,Erchin Serpedin,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PCCA（提示条件通道注意力）的调制机制及其网络框架PROMISE-Net（包含CNN和Transformer两种变体），用于医学图像交互式分割。PCCA通过池化提取紧致通道描述符、投影到共享空间并用门控激励机制融合，实现跨网络层级的提示感知通道注意力权重，从而自适应重校准多阶段特征响应。在四个基准数据集（皮肤病变、息肉、心脏超声、手术器械）上，PROMISE-CNN相对基线U-Net获得0.8%-10.4%的IoU提升，PROMISE-Txformer相对基线UNETR获得1.1%-23.0%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割中，解剖结构模糊、低对比度和模态特异性伪影使自动分割困难。交互式分割虽能提供引导，但现有方法大多在晚期阶段融合提示信息，缺乏在层次化特征表示上进行提示驱动通道维度调制的显式机制，导致无法充分捕捉深层上下文和模态特异性变化。

Method: 提出Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention（PCCA）机制，包含三个核心步骤：（1）通过池化提取紧凑的通道描述符；（2）将提示嵌入和特征描述符投影到共享空间中；（3）通过门控激励机制融合，计算提示感知的通道注意力权重。PCCA可集成到编码器-解码器网络的多个阶段，实现跨层级的提示条件特征重校准。基于此构建PROMISE-Net，分别以卷积模型（PROMISE-CNN）和Transformer模型（PROMISE-Txformer）实例化。

Result: 在ISIC-Lesion、Kvasir-Polyp、CAMUS-Cardiac和Kvasir-Instrument四个基准上，PROMISE-CNN相较于基线U-Net分别获得10.4%、8.7%、0.8%和3.4%的相对IoU提升；PROMISE-Txformer相较于基线UNETR分别获得7.6%、23.0%、2.1%和1.1%的提升。结果在架构、模态和解剖目标上表现出一致改进。

Conclusion: PCCA和PROMISE-Net被证明是一种可扩展、可泛化的提示感知层次化特征调制框架，能够在多种架构、多类成像模态和不同解剖目标下稳定提升医学图像交互式分割性能。

Abstract: Anatomically plausible segmentation remains challenging because of low contrast, ambiguous boundaries, and modality-specific artifacts. Interactive segmentation has emerged as a promising strategy to guide feature extraction and improve localization, particularly in structurally ambiguous regions. However, existing methods integrate prompts through late-stage fusion and lack explicit mechanisms for prompt-driven channel-wise modulation across hierarchical feature representations, limiting their ability to capture deeper contextual and modality-specific variations. To address these limitations, we introduce Prompt-Conditioned Channel Attention (PCCA), a novel modulation mechanism that enables deep, hierarchical integration of semantic prompts within encoder-decoder networks. PCCA extracts compact channel descriptors via pooling, projects them into a shared space, and fuses them through a gated excitation mechanism to compute prompt-aware channel attention weights. These weights adaptively recalibrate feature responses across multiple network stages, enabling prompt-conditioned, semantically enriched hierarchical representations. Building on this, we propose PROMISE-Net, instantiated in two network variants: a convolutional model (PROMISE-CNN) and a transformer-based model (PROMISE-Txformer). Across the ISIC-Lesion, Kvasir-Polyp, CAMUS-Cardiac, and Kvasir-Instrument benchmarks, integrating PCCA into PROMISE-CNN yielded relative IoU gains of 10.4%, 8.7%, 0.8%, and 3.4%, respectively, over the baseline U-Net, while PROMISE-Txformer achieved corresponding gains of 7.6%, 23.0%, 2.1%, and 1.1%, respectively, over the baseline UNETR. These results show consistent improvements across architectures, imaging modalities, and anatomical targets, establishing PCCA and PROMISE-Net as a scalable, generalizable framework for prompt-aware hierarchical feature modulation in medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [121] [Ultra-High-Definition Restoration Transformers with Correlation Matching Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20263)
*Cong Wang,Liyan Wang,Jinshan Pan,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren,Jun Liu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UHDformer++，一个基于Transformer的通用框架，用于解决多种超高分辨率（UHD）图像恢复任务。该框架在四个协调的学习空间中运行：高分辨率（HR）、低分辨率（LR）、超分辨率（SR）和低高融合重建空间（LHFR）。通过FR-CMT和ACM两个模块桥接高低分辨率空间，在至少减少86%模型参数的同时，在5个UHD恢复任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHD图像恢复任务（如低光增强、去雾、去模糊等）对计算和存储资源要求极高，现有方法要么资源开销过大，要么难以在UHD分辨率下兼顾性能与效率。需要一个通用的、参数高效的Transformer框架来同时处理多种UHD图像恢复任务。

Method: UHDformer++包含四个协调的学习空间：HR空间用于多层级特征提取；LR空间用于学习紧凑且具代表性的特征；SR空间将LR特征上采样以为LHFR提供更丰富的特征；LHFR空间用于最终图像恢复。HR提取多尺度高分辨率特征并与LR线索融合产生残差图像，LR从HR中蒸馏互补表征。引入了两个桥接模块：FR-CMT模块从最大池化和均值池化融合的高分辨率特征中选取top C/r个通道替换低分辨率Transformer中信息量较少的通道；ACM模块自适应地重新校准多尺度高分辨率特征，确保只有任务相关信息传播到LR。

Result: 在5个UHD恢复任务（低光增强、去雾、去模糊、去雨、去雪）上的大量实验表明，UHDformer++相比近期最先进方法至少减少86%的模型参数，同时取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过多空间协同设计和通道级特征选择与调制的创新模块，UHDformer++在极大降低模型参数量的前提下，实现了跨多种UHD恢复任务的通用且高性能的解决方案，验证了高-低分辨率特征协同与紧凑特征选择的有效性。

Abstract: We propose UHDformer++, a general Transformer-based framework to solve numerous Ultra-High-Definition (UHD) image restoration tasks. UHDformer++ operates across $4$ coordinated learning spaces: 1) a high-resolution space (HR) for multi-level feature extraction, 2) a low-resolution space (LR) for learning compact, representative features, 3) a super-resolution space (SR) for upsampling low-resolution features from SR, and 4) a low-high fusion and reconstruction space (LHFR) for final image restoration. Specifically, HR extracts multi-scale high-resolution features and fuses them with low-resolution cues to produce residual images, while LR distills complementary representations from HR to improve restoration quality. To supply LHFR with richer features, SR super-resolves LR outputs before fusion. We further introduce two modules to bridge the high- and low-resolution spaces. The Feature-Refined Correlation Matching Transformation (FR-CMT) module selects the top $C/r~(C~\text{denotes the number of channels;~}r\geq1~\text{controls the squeezing level})$, from the fusion between max- and mean-pooled high-resolution features to replace less informative channels in the low-resolution Transformer. The Adaptive Channel Modulator (ACM) adaptively recalibrates multi-scale high-resolution features, ensuring that only task-relevant information propagates to LR. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UHDformer++ reduces model parameters by at least 86\% compared with recent state-of-the-art methods while achieving substantial performance gains across $5$ UHD restoration tasks, including low-light image enhancement, dehazing, deblurring, deraining, and desnowing. Code will be released at https://github.com/supersupercong/uhdformerplus.

</details>


### [122] [Towards Surgical World-Action Modeling: A Preliminary Joint Visual-Trajectory Forecasting for Surgical Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20284)
*Weiliang Huang,Huanrong Liu,Bob Zhang,Qi Dou,Zhen Chen,Yun Gu,Guy Rosman,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合视觉-轨迹世界动作模型，同时预测手术场景未来的视觉状态和器械轨迹，采用分块自回归滚动策略（chunked autoregressive rollout）进行15步预测，验证了联合预测的可行性，但长时程预测仍存在视觉退化和轨迹误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术规划方法将未来场景生成和器械轨迹预测视为两个独立任务，场景模型无法在轨迹层面评估运动准确性，轨迹模型则无法捕捉器械运动的视觉后果。两者割裂导致预测轨迹与未来场景演化之间的一致性未被建模，需要联合预测以提供对手术动作-场景动态的更完整刻画。

Method: 提出初步的联合视觉-轨迹世界动作模型：将历史视频帧和器械轨迹编码为潜在表示，经时空编码器处理后，通过分离的视觉状态预测头和轨迹预测头分别解码。基于该架构，采用分块自回归滚动策略（chunked autoregressive rollout）重复预测未来15个时间步。

Result: 分块策略在所有预测时域上一致优于直接一次性预测，首个片段的PSNR从18.86 dB提升至23.11 dB，平均位移误差（ADE）从45.77像素降至22.22像素，验证了联合视觉-运动预测的初步可行性。

Conclusion: 联合视觉-轨迹预测方法初步可行且分块自回归策略有效，但长时程预测仍存在渐进性视觉退化和轨迹误差累积问题，是未来手术世界动作建模的重要挑战。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models to anticipate not only how instruments will move, but also how the operative visual state will evolve together with such motion. Existing approaches typically treat future scene generation and instrument trajectory prediction as two separate tasks. Scene-only models cannot directly evaluate the accuracy of future instrument motion at the trajectory level, while trajectory-only models fail to capture the visual consequences of instrument movement, leaving the consistency between predicted trajectories and future scene evolution unaddressed. Jointly forecasting both provides a more complete account of surgical action-scene dynamics by enabling explicit trajectory-level evaluation while simultaneously modeling the corresponding visual evolution. To bridge this gap, we present a preliminary joint visual-trajectory world-action model that simultaneously forecasts future visual states and instrument trajectories from historical surgical observations. Specifically, we encode historical video frames and tool trajectories into latent representations, which are processed by a temporal-spatial encoder and subsequently decoded through separate visual-state and trajectory prediction heads. Based on this preliminary architecture, a chunked autoregressive rollout is repeatedly applied to predict fifteen future steps. The chunked strategy consistently outperforms direct one-shot prediction across all evaluated horizons, improving first-segment PSNR from 18.86 to 23.11 dB and reducing ADE from 45.77 to 22.22 pixels. These results demonstrate the initial feasibility of joint visual-motion forecasting. However, we observe progressive visual degradation and accumulated trajectory errors over longer prediction horizons, which remain important challenges for future surgical world-action modeling.

</details>


### [123] [CalcSeg: Confidence-aware 3D Latent Context Curriculum Learning For Myocardial Scar Segmentation From Single-Stack LGE-CMRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20305)
*Nivetha Jayakumar,Hannah Kim,Amit R. Patel,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CalcSeg，一个置信度感知的潜在上下文课程学习框架，用于从单堆栈LGE-CMR图像中进行心肌疤痕分割。该方法通过动态半监督课程学习策略（结合预测误差、认知不确定性和疤痕负荷评估样本难度）以及潜在切片间自注意力机制（从稀疏2D输入推断3D空间表示），在多中心临床数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肌疤痕分割在低组织对比度、弥散性及小面积疤痕区域下具有长期临床挑战性，且单堆栈LGE-CMR成像缺乏3D空间上下文。此外，训练数据有限且标注困难，亟需一种能够自适应处理样本难度并补偿空间信息缺失的鲁棒分割方法。

Method: CalcSeg包含两个核心组件：(1) 动态半监督课程学习策略，利用置信度感知的评分函数（整合预测误差、量化认知不确定性及疤痕负荷估计）自动评估样本难度，无需人工标签，从易到难渐进扩充训练集；(2) 潜在切片间自注意力（latent slice-wise self-attention）机制，从稀疏2D单堆栈输入中推断3D空间表示，补偿空间上下文不足。

Result: 在多中心临床LGE-CMR数据集上，CalcSeg在所有竞争方法中表现一致最优，尤其在临床具有挑战性的病例上取得了显著改善。

Conclusion: CalcSeg通过置信度感知的课程学习与3D潜在表示融合，有效解决了单堆栈LGE-CMR中心肌疤痕分割中空间上下文受限和样本难度不均衡的问题，在临床挑战性场景下展现出优越的鲁棒性和准确性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Myocardial scar segmentation from single-stack late gadolinium-enhanced cardiac magnetic resonance (LGE-CMR) imaging has been a longstanding and clinically important challenge, particularly in the presence of low tissue contrast, diffuse, and small scar regions. These challenges are further intensified by the limited availability of 3D spatial context. This paper presents CalcSeg, a Confidence-aware latent context curriculum learning framework that leverages fused 3D feature representations from single-stack 2D LGE-CMR images for robust scar segmentation. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic semi-supervised curriculum learning strategy that progressively expands training from easier to more challenging scar cases using a learned confidence-aware scoring function. Such a function integrates errors in the predicted scar maps with quantified epistemic uncertainty and scar burden estimation to automatically assess sample difficulty without requiring manual labels. To compensate for the limited spatial context in single-stack acquisitions, we then develop a latent slice-wise self-attention to capture inter-slice dependencies and infer 3D spatial representations from sparse 2D inputs. We evaluate CalcSeg on multi-center clinical LGE-CMR datasets and benchmark against existing scar segmentation networks. Experimental results show that CalcSeg consistently outperforms all competing methods, particularly with substantial improvements on clinically challenging cases. Our code is released on Github.

</details>


### [124] [DreamHand: Repurposing Video Diffusion Models for Occlusion-Robust Egocentric 3D Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20308)
*Yufei Liu,Xixi Wang,Hao Li,Ganlong Zhao,Kaitong Cai,Chengkai Jin,Chunxiao Liu,Jianbo Liu,Siyuan Huang,Xingang Pan,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamHand 将视频扩散模型（VDM）重新用作确定性几何编码器，通过单次前向传播即可暴露遮挡和离屏的手部信息，实现离线片段级别的手部3D轨迹恢复，在五个第一人称基准上刷新SOTA，显著降低MPJPE-p误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频蕴含丰富的可扩展操作数据，但从此类视频中恢复度量级3D手部轨迹极具挑战，主因是严重物体遮挡和频繁的离屏间隙。现有单帧及窗口式时间回归器在手部短暂离开画面时失效，而基于视频扩散模型的方法作为像素级渲染器依赖多步随机采样，计算沉重且不稳定。因此亟需一种既高效又能处理遮挡和离屏场景的手部轨迹恢复方法。

Method: DreamHand 提出离线片段级框架，核心创新包括：1）确定性干净潜变量编码器（Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder），在单次前向中对干净潜变量提取特征，利用扩散模型的场景理解能力暴露当前观测之外（含遮挡与离屏）的手部信息；2）双向时空解码器（Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder）用于解码连续双手轨迹，输出度量级3D定位，无需外部检测器；3）可选基于射线的相机求解器（Ray-Based Camera Solver），支持无需测试时相机内参的第二配置。

Result: 在五个第一人称基准数据集上，DreamHand 均取得SOTA成绩：在遮挡严重的ARCTIC上将MPJPE-p降低30%，在HOT3D上降低40%；当评估中纳入离屏手部时，性能提升扩大至46%-61%。

Conclusion: DreamHand 通过将VDM从随机像素渲染器转化为确定性几何编码器，提供了从日常人类视频到机器人操作数据的高效可扩展路径，在不依赖外部检测器且可免测试时相机标定的情况下，同时突破遮挡和离屏两大难题。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers scalable manipulation data for embodied AI, yet recovering metric 3D hand trajectories remains challenging due to severe object occlusion and frequent out-of-sight gaps. Existing single-frame and windowed temporal regressors fail when hand shortly leaves the frame, while recent video diffusion models (VDMs) rely on heavy, stochastic multi-step sampling as pixel-space renderers. We instead repurpose VDM into a deterministic geometry encoder. A single forward pass over the clean latent exposes scene content beyond current observations, including occluded and out-of-sight hands. We introduce DreamHand, an offline clip-level framework that extracts features via a Deterministic Clean-Latent Encoder and decodes them with a Bidirectional Spatiotemporal Decoder. DreamHand recovers continuous bimanual trajectories with metric placement and no external detector, while a Ray-Based Camera Solver supports a second configuration that needs no test-time camera intrinsics. Across five egocentric benchmarks, DreamHand sets a new state of the art, cutting MPJPE-p by 30% on occlusion-heavy ARCTIC and 40% on HOT3D. These gains reach 46%-61% once out-of-sight hands are included in the evaluation, offering a scalable path from everyday human video to robot manipulation data.

</details>


### [125] [Inter-X++: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Human-Human Interaction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20312)
*Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Zili Lin,Xintao Lv,Yichao Yan,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen,Xiaokang Yang,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Inter-X++，一个大规模高保真人-人交互（HHI）基准数据集，包含11,388条交互序列和超810万帧，配备多模态注释（细粒度文本、交互类别、因果次序、关系与个性、顶点级接触图等），并基于此构建四个类别的生成/感知下游任务统一测试平台，同时提出OpenHHI统一表示与建模框架，在生成与感知任务上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HHI数据集受限于低保真运动学数据、缺乏精细手部动作和丰富的多模态标注；同时交互表示碎片化、评估协议不一致，阻碍了公平严格的基准测试。

Method: 通过新型混合动作捕捉系统采集数据，提供11,388条高保真交互序列及超过810万帧，包含全身运动和精细手指关节。数据配有层级细粒度文本描述、交互类别、因果交互次序、主体关系与个性、顶点级接触图和物理正则化约束等多维标注。据此构建涵盖生成与感知两大范式的四类下游任务统一测试平台，并标准化交互表示和评估协议。进一步提出OpenHHI统一交互表示与建模框架，联合优化交互重建与语义理解。

Result: OpenHHI在生成和感知任务上均取得SOTA性能，实验证明统一表示框架能够同时桥接交互理解与生成。

Conclusion: Inter-X++作为综合性大规模HHI基准有效解决了数据保真度、手部细节、多模态标注和评测标准化等瓶颈；OpenHHI验证了统一的交互表示可以同时促进理解与生成能力，为数字人交互研究提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: The capability to perceive and synthesize human-human interactions is fundamental to developing intelligent digital human systems. However, existing datasets and modeling approaches are fundamentally constrained by low-fidelity kinematics, the omission of dexterous hand gestures and a severe lack of rich multimodal annotations. Furthermore, fragmented interaction representations and inconsistent evaluation protocols also impede fair and rigorous benchmarking. To systematically address these bottlenecks, we present Inter-X++, a comprehensive and large-scale benchmark designed to empower versatile HHI analysis. Captured via a novel hybrid motion capture system, Inter-X++ provides 11,388 high-fidelity interaction sequences and over 8.1M frames, featuring precise whole-body movements and detailed finger articulations. Meanwhile, we enrich the data foundation with multifaceted annotations, including hierarchical fine-grained textual descriptions, interaction categories, causal interaction orders, the relationship and personality of the subjects, as well as vertex-level contact maps and physically regularized constraints. Leveraging these elaborate annotations, we formulate a unified testing ground comprising four categories of downstream tasks that symmetrically span both generative and perceptive paradigms. To eliminate benchmarking ambiguities, we systematically standardize the interaction representations and evaluation protocols. Finally, we go beyond dataset construction to propose OpenHHI, a single and unified HHI representation and modeling framework that jointly optimizes interaction reconstruction and semantic understanding. Extensive experiments reveal that OpenHHI achieves state-of-the-art performance on both generation and perception tasks. This definitively proves that our unified representation successfully bridges interaction understanding and generation simultaneously.

</details>


### [126] [Swift-Image: Exploring the Performance Frontier of Compact Unified Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20334)
*Taihang Hu,Zhao Wang,Zuan Gao,Tao Liu,Hao Yan,Zhengze Xu,Yuhang Yu,Yongchao Du,Xingjian Wang,Jun Zheng,Qinye Zhou,Zhengrui Chen,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Zhengtao Wu,Ge Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Denghui Yang,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Swift-Image 是一个仅有 6B 参数的紧凑型多模态统一模型，支持文本生成图像、单图编辑和多图编辑。通过系统化训练工程（渐进式训练、并行专家强化学习、多教师在线蒸馏、提示增强器、结构化剪枝与少步蒸馏），在仅 243K GPU 训练小时的约束下取得了领先的开源模型聚合性能，并产出了几乎无损的 3B 压缩版本和加速推理变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在有限的算力预算下，通过系统的训练工程（而非堆砌模型规模）能将一个相对较小的视觉生成器推进到何种程度，从而在紧凑参数量的条件下实现与大规模模型相当甚至更优的生成与编辑能力。

Method: 1) 采用高效的 6B 单流 DiT 架构；2) 构建渐进式训练流水线，从宽泛语义覆盖逐步过渡到高分辨率、强视觉质量和统一的生成-编辑监督；3) 后训练阶段使用并行专家强化学习结合多教师在线策略蒸馏，以缓解异构目标之间的干扰；4) 引入 Prompt Enhancer 将高层推理与像素级渲染解耦，将用户请求转化为与生成器对齐的视觉规格；5) 通过结构化剪枝和少步蒸馏得到 3B 和加速变体用于高效部署。

Result: Swift-Image 在仅 6B 参数和 243K GPU 训练小时的情况下，在开源模型中取得领先的聚合性能；压缩后的 3B 模型几乎无性能损失；少步蒸馏在显著减少采样步数的同时进一步提升了聚合编辑性能。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过精心设计的训练工程（架构选择、数据课程、后训练策略、提示增强和模型压缩）可以在紧凑模型规模下实现领先的生成与编辑性能，并总结了可复用的实践经验供后续研究参考。

Abstract: We present Swift-Image, a compact unified model for text-to-image generation, single-image editing, and multi-image editing. Our goal is to explore how far a relatively small visual generator can be pushed through systematic training engineering under a constrained computational budget. Swift-Image adopts an efficient 6B single-stream DiT and a progressive training pipeline that evolves from broad semantic coverage to higher resolution, stronger visual quality, and unified generation-editing supervision. For post-training, we employ parallel expert reinforcement learning followed by multi-teacher on-policy distillation to alleviate interference among heterogeneous objectives. We further decouple high-level reasoning from pixel-level rendering with a Prompt Enhancer that translates user requests into generator-aligned visual specifications. For efficient deployment, structural pruning and few-step distillation produce 3B and accelerated variants. Swift-Image achieves leading aggregate performance among evaluated open-source models with only 6B parameters and 243K GPU training hours; the compressed 3B model incurs nearly no loss, while few-step distillation further improves aggregate editing performance with substantially fewer sampling steps. Our study also summarizes practical lessons for architecture, data curriculum, post-training, prompt enhancement, and model compression.

</details>


### [127] [4DAnyone: Create Anyone in 4D from a Casual Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20335)
*Yudong Jin,Tao Xie,Qihang Zhang,Zehong Shen,Zhen Xu,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DAnyone 是一个从无标定单目视频中重建动态3D人体（4D）的框架。它通过生成多视角一致的视频并提升为4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）来实现。论文指出已有的相机控制视频扩散模型在目标视角数量增多时会失败，将其归因于"有界注意力上下文"问题，并提出参考上下文打包（RCP）和目标上下文路由（TCR）两种互补设计来解决，在多个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制的视频扩散模型虽然能生成合理的全新视角视频，但当目标视角数量扩展到4DGS重建所需的数十个时，无法保持一致性。作者识别出这是"有界注意力上下文"问题：当目标视角超过单个DiT前向传播能力时需分组处理，暴露出两个耦合瓶颈——参考上下文随时间线性增长（O(N)）削弱跨视角外观引导，以及不相连的组间无法直接交换信息导致整体结构漂移。

Method: 4DAnyone提出两个互补设计：1) 参考上下文打包（RCP）：将不断增长的参考视角压缩为固定长度的混合分辨率上下文，使参考上下文复杂度降为O(1)；2) 目标上下文路由（TCR）：在去噪过程中轮换目标视角的分组方式，在高噪声步共享组间上下文，在低噪声步稳定细节。此外，作者用自研游戏引擎构建MVGameHuman数据集，并联合光舞台数据与野外视频数据训练模型。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和DyMVHumans数据集上，4DAnyone在全新视角视频质量以及下游的4DGS重建两方面均优于现有方法，并表现出稳健的野外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 4DAnyone通过解决视频扩散模型在大量目标视角下的注意力上下文瓶颈，实现了从单目视频到高质量4D人体重建的全流程方案，验证了合成数据训练和上下文管理设计的有效性。

Abstract: We present 4DAnyone, a framework for reconstructing 4D humans from an uncalibrated monocular video by generating reconstruction-grade multiview-consistent videos and lifting them into 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS). Existing camera-controlled video diffusion models synthesize plausible novel-view videos but fail to maintain consistency when scaled to the tens of target views required for 4DGS reconstruction. We identify this failure as a bounded-attention-context problem: when target views exceed the capacity of a single DiT forward pass, they must be split into groups, exposing two coupled bottlenecks. On the reference-context side, conditioning on all previously generated views grows as $O(N)$, weakening cross-view appearance guidance. On the target-context side, disjoint groups cannot directly exchange information, causing global structural drift. 4DAnyone addresses both bottlenecks with two complementary designs: Reference Context Packing (RCP) compresses growing reference views into a fixed-length mixed-resolution context with $O(1)$ reference-context complexity, while Target Context Routing (TCR) rotates target-view groupings during denoising to share context across groups at high-noise steps and stabilize details at low-noise steps. We further build the MVGameHuman dataset using our in-house game engine and combine it with light-stage and in-the-wild video datasets for training. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and DyMVHumans show that 4DAnyone outperforms prior methods in both novel-view video quality and downstream 4DGS reconstruction, with robust in-the-wild generalization. See our project page for video results and source code: https://4danyone.github.io.

</details>


### [128] [WithEveryone: Unified Planning and Identity Grounding for Group Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20336)
*Hengyuan Xu,Qixun Wang,Yiji Cheng,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Wei Cheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了WithEveryone框架，用于生成包含多达十个参考身份的人物群像图像。通过注入寻址token、预测结构化的身份-布局计划并渲染为视觉条件，结合Layout-Grounded ID Loss和ID Representation Forcing，实现了高保真的多身份绑定生成，在基准测试中面部相似度从0.462提升至0.499，重复率仅2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需要生成包含多个指定人物的群像场景时，身份保持图像生成变得不可靠——模型不仅要保持每个身份的独特性，还要将每个参考身份绑定到图像中的不同人物和位置。训练时的身份损失需要在多个噪声预测的人脸之间建立对应关系，这使得传统的基于嵌入匹配的方法难以应对。

Method: WithEveryone采用三步框架：(1) 将每个选定的身份注入为带寻址（addressed）的token；(2) 预测结构化的身份-布局计划；(3) 将该计划渲染为视觉条件。核心训练目标为Layout-Grounded ID Loss，利用标注的人脸区域直接监督目标身份，避免不稳定的基于嵌入的人脸匹配；此外还引入ID Representation Forcing，在图像合成前为每个身份训练一个预测。

Result: 在身份不相交的基准测试上，WithEveryone实现了最高的目标-上下文身份相似度，将面部相似度从GPT-Image-2的0.462提升至0.499，同时将复制粘贴伪影从0.169降低至0.055。覆盖97.3%的请求身份，重复率仅为2.8%。

Conclusion: 显式的身份-布局锚定使得身份保持生成能够扩展到更大的群组规模，无需依赖直接的参考人脸复制。

Abstract: Identity-preserving image generation becomes increasingly unreliable when a scene must contain many specified people. Beyond retaining each identity, the model must bind every reference to a distinct person and location, while training-time identity losses must establish correspondence among several noisy predicted faces. We introduce WithEveryone, a unified framework for generating group images up to ten reference identities. WithEveryone injects each selected identity as an addressed token, predicts a structured identity--layout plan, and renders the plan as a visual condition. Its key objective, Layout-Grounded ID Loss, uses annotated face regions to supervise the intended identities directly, avoiding unstable embedding-based face matching; ID Representation Forcing additionally trains a prediction for each identity before image synthesis. On an identity-disjoint benchmark, WithEveryone achieves the highest target-context identity similarity, improving face similarity from 0.462 for GPT-Image-2 to 0.499, while reducing copy-paste artifacts from 0.169 to 0.055. It further covers 97.3\% of the requested identities with a duplicate rate of only 2.8\%. These results show that explicit identity--layout grounding enables identity-preserving generation to scale to larger groups without relying on direct reference-face copying.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Incident-Data Robustness Analysis of the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2026): How a Community-Expert Ranking Holds Up Against a Large-Scale LLM Incident Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19266)
*Kyriakos "Rock" Lambros,Steve Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含7,714个LLM安全事件的大规模语料库，使用贝叶斯测量误差模型校正分类器偏差后，将基于真实事件的排名与OWASP Top 10专家排名进行对比。结果显示两者一致性很弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20），但专家排名依然稳健——四个前沿分类器均无法超越事件基准（平衡准确率0.863），且基准排序与真实标签高度吻合（Spearman ρ=0.918）。这是两位工作组成员的探索性分析，不代表官方OWASP发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications是安全从业者社区共识的专家排名，但作者质疑该专家排名是否与真实世界中的实际安全事件数据吻合。此前缺乏大规模、系统性的真实事件语料库来检验专家共识与经验证据之间的一致性。

Method: 作者从CVE、GHSA、OSV和AIAAIC四个来源收集了7,714个LLM安全事件快照，其中6,639个按20类分类法进行了标注。使用贝叶斯测量误差模型对每个类别的计数进行分类器精确率和召回率校正，得出基于事件的排名。2026年候选名单以固定权重（专家0.75、数据0.25）融合两种信号。并通过预注册的四分类器对比实验（bake-off）验证排名稳健性。

Result: 专家排名与事件数据排名的Cohen's κ≈0.20，且在90%置信区间跨越零，说明两者一致性很弱。四个前沿分类器在预注册对比中均无胜者，没有分类器能超过事件基准（incidence floor）的平衡准确率0.863。基准排序与保留的真实标签数据高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.918）。

Conclusion: 尽管专家排名与真实事件数据的一致性弱，但专家排名本身是稳健的——没有任何分类器能超越简单事件发生率基准，且真实事件排序与基准高度吻合。这意味着OWASP专家排名提供了有价值但与被记录的真实事件存在差异的视角，应保留专家共识的主导权重（0.75），同时用数据（0.25）进行纠正。本文是探索性分析，不替代官方OWASP流程。

Abstract: The OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications ranks the risks that a community of security practitioners judges most important. We ask a narrower question: checked against the record of real incidents, does that expert ranking agree with the data? We assembled a large-scale corpus of LLM-security incidents (7,714 snapshotted and 6,639 labeled against the 20-entry taxonomy) drawn from CVE, GHSA, OSV, and AIAAIC, and derived an incident-based ranking with a Bayesian measurement-error model that corrects each category's count for classifier precision and recall. The 2026 candidate list blends the two signals at fixed weights, 0.75 on the expert vote and 0.25 on the data, so the corpus corrects the consensus without overturning it. The agreement between the two rankings is weak: Cohen's $κ\approx 0.20$, with a 90% interval that crosses zero. The expert ranking is nonetheless robust. A pre-registered bake-off of four frontier classifiers returns no winner. None beats the incidence floor's balanced accuracy of 0.863. A ground-truth check leaves the floor's ordering (Spearman $ρ= 0.918$ against held-out truth) in place. This is an exploratory analysis by two working-group members, not the official OWASP release, and it does not supersede the official list or process.

</details>


### [130] [ABEAT: Efficient and Anonymous Encryption for ABE-based Dynamic Group Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19302)
*Hongmiao Yu,Silas Richelson,Jiachen Chen,K. K. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ABEAT，一种基于属性基加密（ABE）的高效匿名动态群组通信系统，通过新的匿名KP-ABE方案隐藏密文中的明文属性，在保持接收者匿名性的同时将非接收者的解密开销较现有最优方案（如HVE）降低90倍以上，甚至比不提供匿名性的FABEO还低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态群组通信需要灵活高效的多对多通信能力，但在使用属性基加密（ABE）实现群组通信时，接收者的匿名性尚未得到充分保障，这在应急响应等多种场景中至关重要。现有方案在维护接收者匿名性方面存在开销过高的问题。

Method: 提出ABEAT框架，结合基于图的命名空间增强ABE，实现群组的动态形成与变更；提出一种新的匿名KP-ABE（密钥策略属性基加密）方法，在密文中隐藏明文属性以维护接收者匿名性，同时防御多种试图破坏匿名性的攻击，并提供快速的接收者验证机制以降低非接收者的解密开销。

Result: ABEAT将非接收者的解密开销较hidden vector encryption（HVE）等现有最优匿名方案降低超过90倍，甚至比完全不提供匿名性的FABEO方案的解密开销还低40%。

Conclusion: ABEAT在实现接收者匿名性保护的同时，实现了比现有方案（包括非匿名方案）更优的性能，为动态群组通信中的匿名性保障提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential communication among a dynamic group of participants that ensures flexible and efficient many-to- many communication is highly desired capability. We leverage attribute-based encryption (ABE) for confidential group communication and enhance it by a graph-based namespace to create an efficient framework that allows groups to be formed and changed dynamically. In this paper, we focus on the important additional need to maintain the anonymity of recipients of a message, when using ABE for group communication for a variety of usage scenarios (e.g., emergency response).
  We propose ABEAT, an efficient and anonymous dynamic group communication system that also minimizes overhead on receivers who are not the intended recipients of a message. In ABEAT, we propose a new anonymous KP-ABE approach to maintain recipient anonymity. ABEAT hides the clear attribute in the ciphertext of KP-ABE, and also prevents several attacks that seek to break anonymity. ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>


### [131] [Aray: Deterministic-First Synthesis of Benign Artifacts for YARA Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19387)
*Emanuel C. A. Valente,Lourenço A. P. Júnior,Leonardo Gonçalves Chahud,Júlio Cezar Estrella,Marcus Botacin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Aray是一个确定性优先的YARA解释器与正向夹具（positive fixture）合成器，用于在无法获得原始恶意样本的情况下，为YARA规则自动生成合法的匹配文件。在416条公开规则上实现了97.6%的整体通过率，且所有可构造的规则都成功匹配其上游原始规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YARA规则易于分发，但用于演示匹配的恶意软件样本难以共享，这给存储、持续集成、灾难恢复演练和可复现的扫描器验证带来困难。构建替代夹具并非简单地嵌入字面量，因为YARA条件可组合候选、计数、偏移量、整数读取和可执行容器约束，且生成的文件不能复现恶意行为。由于正确性验证是存在性的，只需找到规则匹配集中的任意一个文件成员，而非重建原始样本。

Method: Aray采用确定性优先的设计：模型可以提出建设性归一化或类型化提取回退，但绝不接触后端源码或二进制结构。常规代码负责验证归一化后的规则、推导字符串与整数见证（witnesses），并执行提取、路由、碰撞检查的布局以及ELF、PE或通用序列化。只有残余的归一化语义到达有界模型裁判。整体流程包括归一化接受、可构造性预检、夹具生成和原始规则oracle验证四个环节。

Result: 在416条公开规则条目上，归一化无需模型辅助即接受182条，经模型归一化后共接受234条。可构造性预检接纳406条，且每个被接纳的夹具都匹配其上游原始规则。整体成功率为406/416（97.6%），可构造规则中为406/406，另有十项预期的预检处置，无扫描器不匹配或构造失败。不可达端点确认实现阶段零模型调用。

Conclusion: Aray证明了在无原始样本的情况下，可以通过确定性先验的方法自动合成合法的YARA正面测试文件，且构造流程确定性强、无需模型介入。原始规则oracle用于验证生成夹具匹配源规则；对所有可能文件证明蕴含关系是更强且独立的目标。两个锚定正则表达式的失败在最终运行前已修复，因此这是修复后系统结果而非留出估计。

Abstract: A YARA rule is easy to distribute, but the malware sample used to demonstrate a positive match is not. This complicates storage, continuous integration, disaster-recovery exercises, and reproducible scanner validation. Constructing a replacement fixture requires more than embedding literals: YARA conditions can combine alternatives, counts, offsets, integer reads, and executable-container constraints, while the resulting file should not reproduce malware behavior. Positive validation is existential: it requires one file-level member of a rule's match set, not reconstruction of the originating sample. We present Aray, a deterministic-first YARA interpreter and positive-fixture synthesizer. Models may propose constructive normalizations or typed extraction fallbacks, but never backend source or binary structure. Conventional code validates normalized rules, derives string and integer witnesses, and performs extraction, routing, collision-checked layout, and ELF, PE, or generic serialization. Only residual normalization semantics reach a bounded model judge. We evaluated Aray over 416 public-rule entries. Normalization accepted 182 entries without model assistance and 234 after model normalization. Constructibility preflight admitted 406 entries, and every admitted fixture matched its upstream original rule. This yields 406/416 (97.6%) overall and 406/406 among constructible rules, with ten expected preflight dispositions and no scanner mismatches or construction failures. An unreachable endpoint confirmed zero model invocations during realization. The original-rule oracle validates generated fixtures against their source rules; proving implication for all possible files is a separate, stronger objective. Two anchored-regex failures were repaired before the final run, so these are post-fix systems results, not a held-out estimate.

</details>


### [132] [Redactable blockchains and polynomial equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19401)
*Alexander Demin,Alexey Ovchinnikov,Vladimir Shpilrain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了新的工具来构建具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构，其构造基于多变量多项式方程求逆作为单向函数，目前被认为对量子攻击是安全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的发展，传统的基于数论假设（如大整数分解、离散对数）的密码学方案面临威胁。需要开发具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构，以抵御未来量子攻击。

Method: 提出构建可编辑认证数据结构的新工具，核心思想是将单向函数设计为求解多变量多项式方程（或方程组）。在参数选择合理的情况下，该问题被认为不存在高效的量子求解算法，从而保证后量子安全性。

Result: 成功开发了新的构造工具，其安全性建立在多变量多项式方程求逆的困难性之上，该问题在当前已知量子算法下是安全的。

Conclusion: 基于多变量多项式方程的单向函数为可编辑认证数据结构提供了后量子安全的构造路径，前提是参数选择得当。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>


### [133] [A Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Oral Cancer Screening on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19462)
*Lena D. Swamikannan,Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Jay S. Patel,C. S. Mani,Lakshmi Narayana,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的联邦学习框架，用于口腔癌筛查的协作模型开发。该框架通过Tailscale实现安全网络连接，使用Flower框架进行服务器端聚合，并评估了多种轻量级移动端架构。其中基于MNv4-Conv-S的全局模型（GM-V4）在FedAvg聚合下表现最佳，达到了0.929的AUC和87%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域数据获取受限于法规和患者隐私，口腔临床图像尤其难以获得。联邦学习可以在不集中或共享患者数据的情况下实现跨去中心化数据集的协作模型开发，从而解决数据稀缺问题。

Method: 构建了一个实用的联邦学习框架：客户端通过Tailscale实现安全组网和实时通信，使用Flower框架进行服务器端聚合，客户端部署与编排采用手动配置（未使用企业级FL平台）。评估了MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large和MobileNetV4-Conv-S三种轻量级移动端架构，并使用FedAvg进行全局模型聚合。

Result: 在FedAvg聚合的全局轻量级模型中，基于MNv4-Conv-S的全局模型（GM-V4）性能最优，AUC达到0.929，准确率达到87%。

Conclusion: 该联邦学习框架能够有效支持地理分布式的AI医疗研究者协作，在不共享患者数据的前提下开发出性能优异的口腔癌筛查轻量级模型，验证了在智能手机端部署口腔癌筛查应用的技术可行性。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [134] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS，一种针对联邦学习中梯度反演攻击的轻量级防御方法。通过分析Transformer梯度结构，识别出三条私人令牌信息泄漏通道，并设计三项反向路径操作同时封堵所有通道，在保持模型效用的同时将令牌恢复率降至近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中共享的梯度可被梯度反演攻击恢复私人训练文本，构成严重威胁。现有防御方法最多只处理一条泄漏通道，且要么降低模型效用，要么留下结构信号可被利用。因此需要同时关闭所有分析性泄漏通道的全面防御方案。

Method: 首先通过对Transformer梯度结构的分析，识别出三条令牌信息泄漏通道：注意力输出投影梯度中的低秩子空间（Channel 1）、嵌入梯度行范数稀疏性（Channel 2）、MLP扩展梯度中的可恢复子空间信号（Channel 3）。随后提出AEGIS防御，通过三项无需架构修改的反向路径操作封堵所有通道：冻结注意力投影参数消除Channel 1，向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声破坏Channel 2的令牌存在信号，向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声遮蔽Channel 3。掩码后的梯度同时驱动本地优化器更新和服务器导出，确保两侧均无干净信号。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上评估，AEGIS针对包括分析型和基于优化的各类梯度反演攻击，将令牌恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。对Channel 1和Channel 2提供了形式化保证，并在完全了解机制的自适应对手下进行了经验验证。

Conclusion: AEGIS是首个能同时封堵所有三条分析性泄漏通道的轻量级联邦学习防御方案，在不牺牲模型效用的前提下有效抵御梯度反演攻击，兼具理论保证和实证验证。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [135] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双重混淆方法，通过联合混淆梯度更新信息和训练图像，增强联邦学习对图像恢复攻击（如APRIL）的鲁棒性。该方法结合随机二值权重的梯度扰动技术与图像加密技术，在不显著损失分类性能的前提下，有效减少攻击者可恢复的视觉信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能实现本地数据训练的协作学习，但近年研究表明，共享模型更新可被用于重构训练数据，构成严重的隐私泄露风险。现有单一防御手段（如图像加密或梯度扰动）保护效果有限，需要探索组合策略以更全面地抵御图像恢复攻击。

Method: 提出双重混淆框架：(1) 基于随机二值权重的鲁棒性增强技术，随机将部分梯度元素置零，减少攻击者可获取的原始梯度信息量；(2) 图像加密技术，降低重构图像的视觉可解释性。两者形成互补保护。图像加密允许每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥，避免显式密钥共享。

Result: 在基于Vision Transformer（ViT）的图像分类任务上，该方法在评估设置下能显著减少APRIL攻击恢复出的视觉信息，且除图像加密本身带来的损失外，未造成额外的分类性能退化。

Conclusion: 梯度修改与图像加密的组合具有增强联邦学习隐私保护的潜在价值。尽管该组合不提供绝对的安全保证，但实验结果表明这种多层面防御策略是可行的研究方向。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [136] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT是一个基于信息瓶颈节点引导的自适应APT攻击调查框架，通过图蒸馏、自适应时序图学习和跨时空指纹对齐三个互补组件，在三个DARPA E3数据集上分别达到95.7%、90.9%和88.9%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS减少约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击具有隐蔽性和多阶段性，传统检测方法效果不佳。虽然溯源图提供了丰富的行为上下文，但攻击相关证据稀疏且淹没在大规模常规系统活动中，导致全图学习计算开销大，且难以在长攻击序列中进行关联。

Method: TGL-APT基于攻击相关信息非均匀分布且常由结构上有影响力或行为上独特的实体（信息瓶颈节点）介导的观察，包含三个组件：(1)信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏，抑制溯源冗余同时约束结构失真并保留因果可达性；(2)自适应时序图学习，随节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3)跨时空攻击指纹对齐，关联不同实体和时间窗口中的碎片化可疑活动。最后通过因果扩展和阶段表征重构完整攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上F1分数分别为95.7%、90.9%和88.9%，相比KAIROS减少约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存使用。

Conclusion: TGL-APT有效平衡了检测性能、计算效率和调查能力，为基于溯源图的APT分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [137] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将勒索软件攻击建模为基于搜索的软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，利用遗传算法（GA）在系统活跃度统计偏差的硬约束下优化数据加密过程，使攻击能在数小时而非数分钟内保持隐蔽，并成功规避基于指纹识别的行为监控系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件依赖大规模加密程序，容易被现代防御系统立即检测。现代威胁中存在"持久性缺口"（persistence gap），攻击者希望攻击能在数小时内不被发现而非仅仅几分钟。作者旨在通过将攻击执行形式化为优化问题来解决这一缺口，使攻击在统计上与正常系统行为难以区分。

Method: 将勒索软件执行形式化为搜索式软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，采用遗传算法（GA）优化数据加密策略，施加对系统基线活动统计偏差的硬约束，以进化出能规避行为监控和指纹识别技术的攻击模式。

Result: 实验证明进化出的攻击模式能够成功规避基于指纹识别技术的行为监控系统，表明搜索式方法可以有效生成具有逃避能力的恶意软件。

Conclusion: 搜索式方法为生成逃避型恶意软件提供了强大框架，对自动化软件防御构成了新兴挑战，需要防御方开发新的应对策略。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [138] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MaliciousSkillBench，一个用于恶意 Agent Skill 检测的综合性基准。该基准整合了 13 个公开数据源，将 8,414 条原始恶意记录归一化为 7,539 个唯一身份（分属 4,588 个操作结构族），最终得到 9,740 个 Skill（含 7,505 个恶意和 2,235 个良性）。通过评估三类学习型检测器和三类现成扫描器，发现跨源（Source-Disjoint）评估下检测性能显著下降（F1 从 0.88-0.93 跌至 0.65-0.67），且现有检测器在恶意召回率与良性误报率之间存在严重失衡，表明可靠的恶意 Skill 检测需要更广的跨源覆盖和联合评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills 通过可复用的指令包（可附带脚本、资源和服务配置）为 LLM 智能体提供能力扩展，但这也为恶意行为提供了直接的分发渠道。然而，现有的恶意 Skill 数据集分散在多个来源，存在工件格式、证据体制和良性覆盖不一致的问题，且重复和结构相关的内容使得直接聚合和评估变得困难，亟需一个系统化的综合基准来推动恶意 Skill 检测的研究。

Method: （1）数据整合：合并 13 个公开来源（其中 11 个贡献核心恶意工件），通过归一化将 8,414 条原始恶意记录缩减为 7,539 个唯一身份，并聚类为 4,588 个操作结构族；采用保守的跨标签冲突排除后构成主基准。（2）类别协调：将 11 种攻击类别与 4,983 个恶意身份进行源内映射对齐，以刻画各来源的威胁组成差异。（3）评估：评测三类学习型文本检测器（含 TF-IDF SVM）和三类现成 Skill 扫描器，分别在随机划分、结构不重叠和源不重叠三种评估设定下比较性能。

Result: 学习型检测器在随机划分下达到 0.882-0.932 的 Random Macro-F1，但在 Source-Disjoint 评估下仅 0.653-0.665；最强的 word TF-IDF SVM 在随机/结构不重叠/源不重叠设定下分别得 0.932/0.916/0.665，保持了 95.6% 的恶意召回率，但在未见源上产生 62.4% 的良性误报率。现成扫描器则处于不同的但同样不令人满意的操作区间——降低误报以显著牺牲恶意召回为代价。

Conclusion: 现有检测方法在跨源泛化上表现不足，且攻击检测与良性误报之间存在严重的权衡失衡。可靠的恶意 Skill 检测既需要更广泛的跨源基准覆盖，也需要在设计评估时联合度量攻击检测能力与良性过度标记问题。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [139] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个POSIX兼容的文件系统，利用TEE内的加密承诺和轻量级可信注册表，为机密计算中的持久存储提供端到端完整性和新鲜性保证，防止云提供商对磁盘状态进行回滚、重放、分叉或篡改攻击，且无需修改应用程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算通过TEE保护运行时应用，但持久存储仍然脆弱——即使有磁盘加密，恶意的云提供商仍可对磁盘状态进行回滚、重放、分叉或篡改，破坏有状态应用所需的完整性和新鲜性保证。现有方案要么假设存储可信，要么开销过高，要么需要将完整性逻辑推入应用层。

Method: ShieldFS在TEE内部维护简洁的加密承诺来表征合法的文件系统状态，并将承诺复制到轻量级可信注册表中。磁盘上的数据结构（包括预写日志和存储池）通过哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树进行认证。利用事务和写时复制机制原子性地更新持久化文件系统状态和承诺，读取时验证承诺以检测回滚、重放和双重表态攻击。实现上通过扩展ZFS得到ShieldZFS。

Result: 使用标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载评估，ShieldZFS提供了强完整性和新鲜性保证，性能与最先进文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS成功在机密计算威胁模型下实现了端到端的存储完整性和新鲜性，无需应用修改，且性能开销可控，为机密计算中的持久存储安全提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [140] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath是一种将可验证凭证撤销状态查询移至持有方的方案，通过零知识证明向验证者证明凭证在指定撤销根下未被撤销，同时隐藏可观察元数据。作者首次评估了Verkle树与稀疏Merkle树在凭证撤销场景中的适用性，实验表明更短的认证路径并不必然带来更便宜的零知识证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可验证凭证撤销状态检查可能暴露重复标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可被用作链接不同演示的稳定句柄，破坏持有者的隐私模型。现有方案需要解决如何在验证凭证有效性的同时不泄露可追踪信息的问题。

Method: ShadowPath将撤销状态查询从验证方转移到持有方：每次演示时，持有方在零知识证明中证明其凭证在验证者选定的撤销根下未被撤销。作者同时引入新鲜会话随机数以掩盖状态数据中的可链接性，并对Verkle树（KZG认证）与稀疏Merkle树进行实证比较。

Result: 在30次桌面试验中，Groth16证明的中位时间为：稀疏Merkle树371.6ms，Verkle树2.11s；验证时间分别为3.70ms和7.55ms。两款主流移动设备上Groth16 Verkle证明耗时约3秒。结果显示更短的认证路径并未带来更低的零知识证明成本。

Conclusion: ShadowPath成功将凭证撤销检查移至持有方并保护隐私，但实验表明Verkle树因KZG认证成本较高，在零知识证明场景中并非更优选择。其隐私保证依赖于新鲜会话随机数和会话值独立性假设，且不包括发行方与验证方串通及同步流量场景。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root.
