The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [141] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个标准化指令式LLM分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（含边界案例），并在受控网络安全测试平台上生成了端点特定认证数据集。评估了三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B）与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch统计异常检测。Meta Llama表现最佳（准确率89.3%、F1 91.8%、FNR 11.8%），检测出80%边界异常场景，显著优于Wazuh（准确率52%，FNR 68.6%）和OpenSearch（准确率49.3%，FNR 74.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有日志异常检测方法存在局限：Wazuh依赖预定义规则，无法应对未知威胁；OpenSearch等统计方法仅识别偏离历史模式的行为；LLM方法受提示构建、日志噪声和缺乏端点特定认证数据的影响。本研究旨在解决这些问题，构建一个能检测认证行为异常（包括边界案例）的标准化LLM分类框架。

Method: 开发了一个标准化指令式LLM分类框架，包含：1）构建受控网络安全测试环境以生成端点特定认证数据，产出包含正常、边界和异常三类行为的精选数据集；2）使用统一的ground-truth严重性框架评估三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B）；3）与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch异常检测进行端到端对比。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct取得最佳端到端检测性能：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、假阴性率11.8%，并检测出80%的边界异常场景。Wazuh准确率52.0%、FNR 68.6%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、FNR 74.5%。Wazuh和OpenSearch分别仅检测出20%和15%的边界异常。Qwen具最低平均推理延迟和100%结构化响应有效性；GPT-OSS在有效响应下表现良好。

Conclusion: 指令微调LLM在日志异常检测（尤其是边界案例检测）方面显著优于传统基于规则和统计的方法。Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct是整体性能最优的选择，而Qwen在推理效率和结构化输出可靠性上具有优势。该框架验证了LLM在处理端点特定认证行为异常检测中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [142] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文比较了人类专家与六个预训练检测器在检测深度伪造语音方面的表现，发现随着合成语音技术的进步（从2019年RTVC到2024年ElevenLabs），人类检测F1分数从约90%骤降至48%；在部分伪造场景下，人类严格准确率仅9%，且77%的时间将伪造句误判为真实。人类与检测器以互补方式失败，均无法可靠定位短时篡改，且人类倾向于将真实语音误标为伪造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究报道人类检测深度伪造语音准确率为70-80%，但这些研究主要基于较旧的语音合成器。随着语音合成技术（如ElevenLabs）在2019至2024年间飞速进步，旧结论可能严重高估了人类的检测能力，亟需在更现代、更真实的场景下重新评估人类与自动检测器的表现。

Method: 选取2019年（RTVC）、2022年（YourTTS）和2024年（ElevenLabs）三种代表性语音合成工具，招募82名IT专业人员作为被试；同时使用六个预训练深度伪造检测器在同一材料上进行基准对比。实验包含全合成语音（full spoofs）和部分伪造（partial spoofing，仅改动一句话）两种条件，所有被试均被明确告知存在深度伪造。

Result: 全合成语音条件下，人类检测F1分数从RTVC/YourTTS的约90%降至ElevenLabs的48%；部分伪造条件下，严格准确率降至9%，且77%的情况下被试将合成句误判为真实。人类与检测器的错误模式互补，均无法可靠定位短时篡改；同时人类对真实语音的误判率上升，导致对未处理音频的信任度下降。

Conclusion: 人类感知在现代语音合成和部分伪造条件下不可靠，仅有警告提示不足以提升检测能力。研究中人类与检测器的互补失败模式表明，需要结合程序化验证、来源追踪、水印技术以及片段级别的检测方法来应对深度伪造语音的威胁。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [143] [COPA: Continual Preference Optimization for Adaptive Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19982)
*Roshan Sood,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: COPA是一个持续偏好优化框架，将提示注入防御视为终身学习问题，通过GRPO优化和边际加权经验回放，实现对自适应对手的持续适应，同时缓解灾难性遗忘并保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的提示注入防御方法大多是静态的，依赖固定的对齐目标或特定攻击的过滤机制，当新攻击策略出现时需要重新设计。已有的终身对齐方法虽然能处理用户偏好变化，但无法应对不断演化以利用已学防御弱点的自适应对手。在实际部署中，攻击分布持续演变，需要在不牺牲对已遇到威胁防御能力的前提下持续适应。

Method: COPA框架将提示注入防御建模为终身学习问题。它采用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行增量优化，从新观察到的攻击中持续吸收反馈；同时引入边际加权经验回放（margin-weighted experience replay）机制，保留对先前攻击类别的防御能力，从而在适应新威胁的同时缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在终身提示注入攻击流的实验中，与最先进的防御方法相比，COPA将攻击成功率（ASR）平均降低了最多6.3倍（最高场景）和4.4倍（平均场景）。

Conclusion: 持续偏好优化是防御LLM免受自适应对手攻击的有效范式，能够在动态威胁环境下保持稳健的防御能力，同时不损害模型的一般功能。

Abstract: LLMs remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, where adversarial instructions embedded in user inputs or external content manipulate model behavior and bypass safeguards. Existing defenses are predominantly static, relying on fixed alignment objectives or attack-specific filtering mechanisms that require redesign as new attack strategies emerge. While recent lifelong alignment methods address shifting user preferences, they do not account for adaptive adversaries that continually evolve to exploit weaknesses in previously learned defenses. This limitation is particularly important in real-world deployments, where evolving attack distributions necessitate continual adaptation without sacrificing robustness to previously encountered threats. We present COPA, a continual preference optimization framework that treats prompt-injection defense as a lifelong learning problem. Instead of one-time alignment, COPA incrementally incorporates feedback from newly observed attacks via GRPO-based optimization and uses margin-weighted experience replay to retain defenses against prior attack classes. This enables continuous adaptation to emerging threats while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and preserving general-purpose model capabilities. Across lifelong prompt injection attack streams, COPA reduces attack success rate by up to 6.3x and 4.4x on average compared to state-of-the-art defenses. These results highlight continual preference optimization as an effective paradigm for defending LLMs against adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [144] [EchoCoT: Extracting Hidden Chain-of-Thought from Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20055)
*Yiting Qu,Ziqing Yang,Chi Cui,Ye Leng,Junjie Chu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoCoT攻击方法，通过利用工具调用之间的"推理回放面"，能够从黑盒大型推理模型（LRMs）中近乎逐字地提取隐藏的思维链（CoT）。在开源模型上实现了高达66.4%的近似逐字提取成功率，在Gemini-2.5上成功提取了33,463 token的超长CoT，揭示了隐藏CoT提取作为一个实际安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿专有大型推理模型（LRMs）的隐藏思维链（CoT）追踪是极具价值的模型资产，但此前关于能否通过API交互从黑盒模型中直接提取这些隐藏CoT的研究几乎空白。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地研究隐藏CoT被提取的现实风险。

Method: 作者首先识别了一个此前被忽视的"推理回放面"——即工具调用之间的间隙区域。基于此开发了EchoCoT，一个多步攻击方法，利用API返回的保真度信号迭代提取隐藏CoT。此外，构建了一个基于LLM的优化框架，自动搜索适用于不同数据集的通用注入轨迹。

Result: 在三个开源和五个前沿专有LRM上进行评估。开源模型上EchoCoT实现高达66.4%的近似逐字提取成功率（提取轨迹长度在目标10%以内，且至少90%的token与目标CoT完全匹配），同一注入轨迹可泛化到未见数据集，提取成功率达80%。专有模型上，大量提取的CoT与提供商报告的推理长度和可用的CoT摘要高度吻合。在Gemini-2.5上成功提取33,463 tokens（目标为32,948 tokens）的超长CoT。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了隐藏CoT提取是一个实际的安全风险，强调了需要更好地保护隐藏CoT资产的重要性。

Abstract: Hidden chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, especially those from frontier proprietary large reasoning models (LRMs), are valuable model assets. Yet whether these hidden CoTs can be directly extracted from black-box models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically study whether hidden CoTs can be extracted near-verbatim from black-box LRMs through API interactions. We identify a previously overlooked reasoning replay surface between tool calls and develop EchoCoT, a multi-step attack that iteratively extracts hidden CoTs using API-returned fidelity signals. We further develop an LLM-based optimization framework that automatically searches for an effective universal injection trajectory across various datasets. We evaluate EchoCoT on three open-source and five frontier proprietary LRMs. On open-source LRMs, EchoCoT achieves up to 66.4\% near-verbatim extraction success, with the extracted trace length within 10\% of the target and at least 90\% of tokens exactly matching the target CoT. The same injection trajectory also generalizes to unseen datasets, achieving up to 80\% extraction success under the same criterion. For tested frontier proprietary LRMs, a substantial fraction of extracted CoTs closely align with provider-reported reasoning lengths and available CoT summaries. EchoCoT can also extract very long CoTs: on Gemini-2.5, it extracts 33,463 tokens from a 32,948-token target. These results establish hidden-CoT extraction as a practical security risk and highlight the need to better protect hidden CoT assets.

</details>


### [145] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会和区块链的检索增强生成系统，通过零知识协议让领域专家委员会认证文档，利用安全多方计算聚合隐藏评分生成可验证的信任分数，并通过哈希承诺跨链维护文档与分数，确保任何文档或评分不可被篡改或丢弃，且每个排序都可独立重放验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖集中式数据库，缺乏对文档来源、完整性和可信度的监督，在医疗、金融、物流和法律等领域，错误或被操纵的文档可能导致严重决策失误，因此需要为RAG引入可验证的信任机制。

Method: TrustRAG采用委员会认证机制，首先由领域专家组成的委员会通过零知识协议对文档进行认证；专家隐藏评分通过安全多方计算（MPC）聚合为客户端可验证的信任分数；文档数据和评分通过跨链哈希承诺维护，确保不可篡改，且排序可独立重放验证。

Result: 论文提出了TrustRAG的完整架构设计，解决了RAG系统中文档来源验证、防篡改和可信度评估等核心问题，实现了文档认证、信任评分和排序验证的全链路可审计性。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过结合零知识证明、安全多方计算和区块链技术，为RAG系统构建了一个去中心化、可验证、防篡改的信任基础设施，适用于对文档时效性和准确性要求极高的关键领域。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [146] [A Meta-Study on Replication Papers in Usable Security & Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20108)
*Christian Mack,Benjamin Berens,Hanna Algedri,Tobias Hilt,Daniela Reimer,Peter Mayer,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法对可用性安全与隐私领域（2016-2025年）的复制研究现状进行了系统分析：审查13个学术场所的征稿启事、系统搜索得到24篇相关出版物、使用Olszewski等人(2025)的分类法进行归类，并对这些论文的作者进行问卷调查。研究发现四个关键洞见：征稿启事需要更清晰的复制研究指南、复制研究的修改内容难以识别、样本中不存在严格意义上的精确复制（约三分之二的论文修改了原始研究的多个方面）、时间和情境变化对结果的影响是最常被引用的复制动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用性安全与隐私研究是一个年轻且快速扩展的领域，但尚未建立成熟的研究标准（如复制研究的标准）。作者旨在了解该领域复制研究的现状，以推动领域内研究规范的完善。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：(1) 审查13个安全、隐私和人机交互领域学术场所的Call for Papers；(2) 系统性搜索2016-2025年间报告复制用户研究的24篇论文；(3) 运用Olszewski等(2025)的复制分类法对这24篇论文进行分类；(4) 对论文作者发放问卷以了解其复制动机。

Result: 四个关键发现：(A) 征稿启事需要为作者和审稿人提供更清晰的复制研究指南；(B) 阅读复制论文时难以确定对原始研究的修改内容；(C) 样本中不存在严格的精确复制，约三分之二的论文对原始研究进行了多方面的修改；(D) 时间和情境变化对结果的影响是复制研究最常被引用的动机之一。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，作者为学术场所、研究者和同行评审者提供了加强可用性安全与隐私领域复制研究实践的实际建议。

Abstract: The field of usable security and privacy research is a young and expanding field, which is still developing standards for its research, e.g. regarding replications. We used a mixed-method approach, in order to get a better understanding of the current state of replications in the field of usable security and privacy: (1) we examine the Call for Papers of 13 venues spanning security, privacy, and human-computer interaction; (2) we conduct a systematic search for papers reporting replicated user studies published across these venues between 2016 and 2025, yielding 24 relevant publications; (3) we categorized these 24 papers employing the replication taxonomy proposed by Olszewski et al. (2025); (4) we distributed a survey to the authors of these papers to understand their motivations for conducting replications. Our analysis reveals four key insights: (A) Calls for Papers would benefit from clearer guidelines for authors and reviewers regarding replication work; (B) determining what modifications were made relative to the original study proves difficult when reading replication papers; (C) strict exact replications do not exist in our sample. Approximately two-thirds of the 24 studies altered multiple aspects of the original work; (D) temporal and contextual changes affecting results emerged as one of the most frequently cited motivations for replication. Based on these findings, we offer practical recommendations for venues, researchers, and peer reviewers to strengthen replication practices in usable security and privacy research.

</details>


### [147] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，使CellCnn卷积神经网络能够在秘密共享数据上完成训练和推理，在不暴露原始患者数据的情况下识别稀有疾病相关细胞亚群，并在CMV和AML基准数据集上保持接近明文版本的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有疾病相关细胞亚群的检测对理解白血病和病毒感染等疾病至关重要，但训练可靠的CellCnn模型需要比单一机构所能汇集更大更多样化的患者队列。然而单细胞数据过于敏感，在现有隐私法规下无法跨机构共享，因此需要一种在保护隐私前提下实现模型训练和推理的框架。

Method: 提出一个安全多方计算（MPC）框架，使CellCnn能够完全在秘密共享数据上进行训练和推理。该方法确保参与方和计算服务器均无法观察到原始患者数据或中间值。与先前移除ReLU激活和偏置项等组件来简化隐私保护的做法不同，本方法保留了CellCnn架构中的全部关键组件。

Result: 在巨细胞病毒感染（CMV）和急性髓系白血病（AML）的基准单细胞数据集上评估，该方法保持了接近明文对应版本的准确率，同时优于先前基于隐私保护的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该MPC框架在保护患者数据隐私的前提下，成功保留了CellCnn的完整架构（包括ReLU激活和偏置项），实现了接近明文精度的性能，为跨机构协作训练稀有细胞亚群检测模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values. Evaluated on benchmark single-cell datasets for cytomegalovirus infection (CMV) and acute myeloid leukaemia (AML), our implementation preserves accuracy close to its plaintext counterpart while outperforming the prior privacy-preserving baseline. In contrast to earlier privacy-preserving approaches that removed components such as ReLU activations and bias terms, our method retains these key parts of the CellCnn architecture and supports accurate analysis without exposing raw patient data.

</details>


### [148] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链的信任管理机制，用于解决移动边缘网络中恶意边缘服务器、自私移动节点及缓存资源受限等问题。该机制通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任值，结合激励机制促进缓存共享，并利用区块链认证机制防止节点冒充，同时在共识过程中考虑移动性、能耗和计算能力约束。仿真结果表明该方法即使在10%数据噪声下也能准确区分诚实节点与恶意节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络流量持续增长，边缘服务器内容缓存对降低延迟至关重要，但面临两大问题：一是恶意边缘服务器可能删除或篡改缓存内容；二是边缘服务器缓存容量有限。为克服容量限制，可借助helper移动节点贡献缓存资源，但节点有自私行为需要激励机制，且这些helper节点同样可能为恶意节点。

Method: 提出区块链驱动的信任管理机制：(1) 利用智能合约计算直接信任和间接信任，有效过滤恶意节点；(2) 基于移动节点对服务质量的满意度判定可信度，信任数据安全存储在区块链上；(3) 引入奖励机制激励节点共享缓存以对抗自私行为；(4) 基于区块链的认证机制防止节点身份冒充；(5) 在共识过程中综合考虑移动节点移动性、能耗和计算能力约束，优化信任、缓存容量与成本效率。

Result: 仿真结果显示，所提方法即使在数据包含10%噪声的情况下，仍能准确区分诚实节点与恶意服务器。

Conclusion: 该区块链信任管理机制能有效应对移动边缘缓存网络中的恶意节点、自私行为和容量受限问题，在存在数据噪声的复杂环境中仍保持高精度的信任评估能力。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [149] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Chameleon，一种基于多对多随机流量变形的网站指纹（WF）防御方法。通过高类内多样性和低类间差异选择变形候选，并利用基数树同步机制支持Tor可插拔传输端点的一致性变形轨迹识别，显著降低对抗训练和防御感知自编码器（DAAE）攻击的有效性，同时大幅减少带宽和时间开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数WF防御方法会创建可学习的网页流量映射特征，且作者证明能够抵抗对抗训练的防御不一定能抵抗基于DAAE的攻击。这揭示了现有防御在对抗更强攻击者时的根本性弱点，需要一种能增加攻击者不确定性的新型防御方案。

Method: Chameleon采用多对多随机流量变形策略：选取高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选，将每个网页流量随机映射到多个候选目标，并允许不同网页共享变形目标以增加攻击者的不确定性。为支持实际Tor部署，设计了基于基数树（radix-trie）的同步机制，利用包方向前缀使可插拔传输（PT）端点识别一致的变形轨迹，并结合轨迹变异和归一化前缀匹配降低开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、六种先进防御和五种WF攻击的闭集/开集设置下评估：与Adaptive Tamaraw相比，Chameleon将对抗训练攻击精度降低高达36.74%，同时带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%。在GTT23上的DAAE攻击下，Chameleon将攻击F1-score限制在35.19%，而Adaptive Tamaraw仅能限制到88.22%。真实PT桥接环境中仅引入16.25%的时间开销。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过多对多随机流量变形机制有效解决了现有WF防御中可学习映射特征和DAAE攻击脆弱性的问题，在显著降低强攻击有效性的同时保持较低的开销，证明了其在实际Tor部署中的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>


### [150] [VQC-ZTI: Variational Quantum Control for Zero Trust Protection of the Tactile Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18572)
*Mubassir Serneabat Sudipto,Shakil Ahmed,Ashfaq Khokhar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VQC-ZTI框架，采用分裂平面变分量子分类器为触觉互联网服务提供零信任保护。离路径VQC分析加密流遥测数据，在路径策略引擎应用缓存的确定性授权、限制、升级和拒绝动作。在CESNET导出数据上，混合量子神经网络在三种划分方式下AUC达0.9941-0.9981，相比ExtraTrees将误报率降低44.6%-67.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉互联网将网络事件直接关联到物理执行，安全决策需在不干扰控制路径的前提下提升风险判别能力。现有方法无法在保持控制路径可预测性的同时实现更精准的安全检测，因此需要一种能在离路径进行异常评分、在路径仅执行确定性策略的安全框架。

Method: VQC-ZTI采用分裂平面架构：离路径的变分量子分类器（VQC）分析加密流遥测数据进行异常评分，在路径上的策略引擎基于缓存结果应用确定性动作（授权、限制、升级、拒绝）。异常评分与策略执行解耦，使检测器灵敏度和策略激进程度可独立调节。实现采用PyTorch-PennyLane混合框架，在CESNET派生聚合流量上使用随机、实体分组和时间划分三种方式评估。

Result: 全混合量子神经网络在随机、实体分组和时间划分下平均AUC分别为0.9981、0.9974和0.9941，相比ExtraTrees将误报率分别降低44.6%、49.6%和67.9%。组件时序分解表明批量VQC评分保持在异步证据路径而非即时执行路径。

Conclusion: VQC-ZTI通过将量子异常检测与确定性策略执行解耦，在不扰动控制路径的前提下显著提升了触觉互联网零信任安全的风险判别能力，且验证了VQC评分可保持在异步路径中，不增加即时执行延迟。

Abstract: Tactile Internet services couple cyber events directly to physical actuation, so security decisions must improve risk discrimination without perturbing the control path. This paper presents VQC-ZTI, a split-plane Variational Quantum Classifier framework for zero-trust protection of Tactile Internet services, in which an off-path VQC analyzes encrypted-flow telemetry while an on-path policy engine applies cached deterministic grant, restrict, step-up, and deny actions. By decoupling anomaly scoring from enforcement, VQC-ZTI preserves predictable control behavior and allows detector sensitivity and policy aggressiveness to be tuned independently. We evaluate the framework on CESNET-derived aggregated traffic using random, entity-group, and temporal holdouts with a hybrid PyTorch-PennyLane implementation. The full-hybrid Quantum Neural Network achieves mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.9981, 0.9974, and 0.9941 and reduces the false-positive rate relative to ExtraTrees by 44.6%, 49.6%, and 67.9%, respectively. A representative component-timing decomposition further illustrates that batched VQC scoring remains in the asynchronous evidence path rather than the immediate enforcement path.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [TorchDCM: A Unified PyTorch-Native Package for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19231)
*Baichuan Mo,Zhengzhong Ricky You,Xiqun Michael Chen,Ruimin Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文介绍TorchDCM，一个基于PyTorch的开源离散选择模型估计Python包。它通过编译选择数据和模型规范为统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎，覆盖Biogeme和Apollo的主要计量功能（多项、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量和面板似然等），支持CPU/CUDA。在7个其他估算包的对比实验中，TorchDCM完成所有45个合成案例且速度最快，相比Biogeme和Apollo将中位运行时间降低89.1%-99.7%，CUDA额外提供12-71倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCM软件提供成熟的计量工作流（如Biogeme、Apollo），而近年GPU导向工具仅加速少数选定模型，在计量经济学覆盖范围和可扩展的可微计算之间留有空白。需要一个既能覆盖广泛计量功能又能利用GPU可微计算的统一工具。

Method: TorchDCM将选择数据和模型规范编译为统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎，支持CPU和CUDA设备上的估计、推断、预测和结构化报告。覆盖Biogeme和Apollo的主要计量功能，包括多项、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量和面板似然，并支持不规则选择集、参数约束、协方差估计、支付意愿分析、弹性以及可扩展的似然组件。通过将模型编译为可微计算图实现自动微分和GPU并行加速。

Result: 在与七个其他估计包的对齐合成和真实数据全估计实验中，TorchDCM完成全部45个合成案例，在每个可比合成案例中运行最快，并在每个至少有2个可比解决方案的比较中满足预设的最终对数似然容差。相比Biogeme和Apollo，中位运行时间降低89.1%-99.7%；CUDA相比单核TorchDCM提供额外的12.0-71.0倍加速。

Conclusion: TorchDCM在保持完整计量经济覆盖的同时，实现了显著的速度提升和可重复性，为计量经济学估计和可微选择模型开发建立了可扩展的基础。该包已开源并提供可执行示例。

Abstract: Estimating large and simulation-intensive discrete choice models (DCMs) requires repeated evaluation of utilities, probabilities, derivatives, and simulated likelihoods over many observations, alternatives, and draws. Existing DCM software provides mature econometric workflows, while recent GPU-oriented tools accelerate selected models, leaving a gap between econometric coverage and scalable differentiable computation. We introduce TorchDCM, an open Python package for discrete choice modeling that compiles choice data and model specifications into a unified PyTorch-native likelihood engine for estimation, inference, prediction, and structured reporting on CPU or CUDA devices. The package covers the principal econometric functionality available across Biogeme and Apollo, including multinomial, nested, mixed, ordered, latent-variable, and panel likelihoods. It also supports ragged choice sets, constrained parameters, covariance estimation, willingness-to-pay analysis, elasticities, and extensible likelihood components. We evaluate TorchDCM against seven other estimation packages in aligned synthetic and real-data full-estimation experiments. TorchDCM completes all 45 synthetic cases, runs fastest in every comparable synthetic case, and satisfies the prespecified final-log-likelihood tolerance in every comparison with at least two comparable solutions. More precisely, it reduces median runtime by 89.1%-99.7% relative to Biogeme and Apollo across model-data settings. CUDA provides an additional 12.0-71.0x speedup over single-core TorchDCM. These results establish a scalable and reproducible foundation for econometric estimation and differentiable choice-model development. The open-source package and executed examples are available at https://github.com/mbc96325/torchdcm.

</details>


### [152] [Discrete Diffusion Inference-Time Control with Nested Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20123)
*Lohithsai Yadala Chanchu,Hany Abdulsamad,Christian A. Naesseth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛方法（NSMC及其全自适应变体FA-NSMC）应用于离散扩散语言模型的推理期控制，用于在无需重新训练的代价下将采样导向序列级奖励，并在毒性和流畅性控制任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理期控制方法（如best-of-$n$采样和bootstrap SMC）分别存在过度乐观和权重退化问题，且此前NSMC公式存在偏差性错误，需要更稳健的方法来引导离散扩散语言模型的采样过程以满足序列级奖励约束。

Method: 作者将嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛（NSMC）和全自适应嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛（FA-NSMC）公式化用于Feynman-Kac导向采样，识别并纠正了先前NSMC公式中导致最终估计有偏的错误，应用于离散扩散语言模型的推理期文本生成控制。

Result: 在毒性和流畅性控制任务上，NSMC和FA-NSMC一致性地优于best-of-$n$采样和bootstrap SMC方法，展示了更强的推理期控制性能。

Conclusion: 嵌套SMC方法有效克服了现有粒子方法在离散扩散语言模型推理期控制中的局限性，为序列级奖励约束下的文本生成提供了更可靠的推理期控制方案。

Abstract: We study inference-time control for text generation in discrete diffusion language models, where the goal is to steer sampling toward sequence-level rewards without retraining. Prior work in this domain has focused on particle-based methods such as best-of-$n$ sampling and bootstrap sequential Monte Carlo, which may suffer from overoptimism and weight degeneracy, respectively. We address these limitations using \emph{nested} sequential Monte Carlo methods. We formulate nested SMC (NSMC) and fully-adapted nested SMC (FA-NSMC) for Feynman--Kac steering, identifying and correcting errors in prior formulations that lead to biased final estimates. We evaluate these methods on toxicity and fluency steering tasks, showing that NSMC and FA-NSMC consistently outperform best-of-$n$ and bootstrap SMC.

</details>


### [153] [Transfer Learning in Nonparametric Regression with Deep ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20255)
*Junpeng Ren,Carlos Misael Madrid Padilla,Yanzhen Chen,Oscar Hernan Madrid Padilla*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多组非参数回归的通用迁移学习框架，采用两阶段偏移学习策略：先合并所有组数据估计总体均值函数，再估计各组的偏移量，最终通过加法组合得到组级估计器。在温和的复杂度和噪声条件下建立了L2误差上界，并以深度ReLU网络为例推导了层次组合模型下的显式收敛速率，证明能够克服维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非参数回归中常存在多个数据组（如不同人群或不同实验条件），但这些组往往共享某些结构。现有方法要么忽略组间共性分别建模，要么简单合并数据忽略组间差异。本文旨在利用组间共享结构实现迁移学习，在提升各组估计精度的同时保留组间个性化特征，解决高维非参数估计中的维度灾难问题。

Method: 采用两阶段偏移学习（two-stage offset learning）框架。第一阶段将所有组的数据池化，估计一个总体均值函数；第二阶段利用各组数据分别估计各自的偏移函数；最终将总体均值函数与各组偏移相加得到各组最终估计器。各组间的共享结构以加法形式分离为公共部分和组特有的偏差部分。

Result: 在温和的复杂度和噪声条件下，为所提出的框架建立了L2误差的上界。当采用深度ReLU网络实现时，在层次组合模型下推导出显式收敛速率，证明算法可克服维度灾难。分析了能够实现正迁移（即更快收敛速率）的条件，包括利用更简单函数进行学习和通过池化样本实现数据增强的场景。仿真和真实数据实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文的转移学习框架有效利用了多组数据间的共享结构，通过加法形式的偏移学习实现组间知识迁移，在理论上保证了L2收敛速率，并能克服高维非参数回归中的维度灾难，具有理论和实证上的双重有效性。

Abstract: This paper develops a general transfer learning framework for nonparametric regression with data consisting of multiple groups. Under the assumption that groups share a common structure along with group-specific deviations in additive form, the proposed method employs a two-stage offset learning procedure: the first stage pools data from all groups to estimate an overall mean function, and the second stage estimates offsets for each group, yielding final group-level estimators through additive combination. Upper bounds on the $\mathcal L_2$ error are established for the proposed framework, covering a broad class of nonparametric estimators under mild complexity and noise conditions. When instantiated with deep ReLU networks, explicit convergence rates are derived under hierarchical composition models, demonstrating the ability to overcome the curse of dimensionality. Conditions that enable positive transfer with faster rates are considered, including learning with simpler functions and data augmentation through pooling samples across groups. Various simulations and real-data experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Tau-Rho Equality and Other Dependence Measures of a Subclass of Factorizable Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19608)
*Noppawit Yanpaisan,Tippawan Santiwipanont,Matthias Scherer,Songkiat Sumetkijakan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kendall's tau与Spearman's rho这两个依赖度量何时相等的问题。作者证明，对于所有形如Ce,α∗Cβ,e的可因式化copula，其中α和β是分段线性单调满射（PLMS），tau等于rho。进一步研究了Chatterjee秩相关系数和尾部依赖系数，发现了一些代数公式和意想不到的现象，包括Chatterjee系数可能表现出极端的非对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kendall's tau和Spearman's rho是统计和风险管理中广泛使用的两种依赖度量，虽然常被视作可互换，但二者可能产生显著分歧。已有研究（Schreyer et al., 2017）刻画了可达到的(τ,ρ)对的精确区域，但关于两者何时相等这一互补性问题尚未得到系统研究。识别使tau=rho成立的非平凡copula族具有理论意义和应用价值。

Method: 研究的copula族具有特殊形式：Ce,α∗Cβ,e，即由分段线性单调满射（PLMS）函数α和β通过因子化（factorizable）构造生成的copula。作者在该族中证明tau=rho的成立，并进一步推导依赖度量（包括Chatterjee秩相关系数和尾部依赖系数）在这一族上的代数表达式。使用的方法包括PLMS构造、copula的因式分解以及对这些度量的解析计算。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明了所有PLMS生成的可因式化copula Ce,α∗Cβ,e满足tau_C=rho_C；(2) 获得了Chatterjee秩相关系数和尾部依赖系数在这些copula上的代数公式；(3) 发现了意外现象：Chatterjee系数可以表现出极端的非对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作补充了对tau与rho不一致性的理解，通过识别tau=rho成立的非平凡copula族，丰富了对依赖度量的认识。Chatterjee系数的极端非对称性提示该度量与经典关联度量在行为上存在本质差异，值得在使用时注意。

Abstract: Kendall's tau and Spearman's rho, two widely used dependence measures in statistics and risk management, are often treated as interchangeable, yet can disagree sharply: Schreyer et al.~(2017) established the exact region of attainable $(τ,ρ)$ pairs. We study the complementary question of equality, namely, identifying nontrivial families of copulas $C$ satisfying $τ_C=ρ_C$. We prove that this equality holds for every factorizable copula of the form $C_{e,α}\ast C_{β,e}$, where $α$ and $β$ are piecewise linear monotonic surjections (PLMS). For these PLMS-generated copulas, we study further dependence measures, including Chatterjee's rank correlation coefficient and tail dependence coefficients, revealing some useful algebraic formulas and unexpected phenomena. In particular, Chatterjee's coefficient can exhibit extreme asymmetry.

</details>


### [155] [Trustworthy Decisions in Reliability Set Estimation under Insufficient Model Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19815)
*Holger Dette,Zhengfu Liu,Jun Yu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的框架，将不完美的"工作模型"转化为可信赖的可靠性集合估计决策规则。框架包含三个部分：建模后校准流程（通过非对称代理损失和校准集选择偏置校正阈值，实现O_P(1/n)体积收敛）、利用共形风险控制来保证最关键的假包含风险在任何工作模型质量下都被控制在预置水平、以及自适应设计将采样集中在可靠性集合及其边界处以提升模型质量。实验表明该方法不仅集合估计更准确，还能提供经典plug-in方法所缺乏的有限样本风险控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠性集合估计在安全关键决策中至关重要，但实际应用中通常只有一个不完美的"工作模型"来近似真实响应曲面。直接依赖这个不完美模型可能产生决策风险，例如将不安全的区域错误认证为安全的。因此需要一种统一框架，能够将不完美的工作模型转化为可信赖的决策规则。

Method: 本文提出三部分框架：(1) 建模后校准流程——将估计与决策解耦，引入非对称可观测的代理损失，利用独立的校准集选择偏置校正阈值以减少决策风险；(2) 利用共形风险控制（conformal risk control）与代理损失结合，在任何工作模型质量下将最安全关键的"假包含"风险控制在预置水平；(3) 自适应实验设计，将观测集中部署在可靠性集合及其边界区域，在错误最影响决策的地方改善模型质量，同时在其他区域控制预算。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法不仅比经典plug-in方法获得更准确的集合估计，还实现了校准的有限样本风险控制——这是plug-in方法所缺乏的。此外，理论分析证明了O_P(1/n)的体积收敛速率，并论证了单独的校准集对于风险控制的必要性。

Conclusion: 本文确认了以下要点：建模后校准可以降低决策风险并达到快速体积收敛；共形风险控制能够在不依赖工作模型质量的情况下提供有限样本的假包含风险保证；单独的校准集是实现风险控制的必要条件；自适应设计能进一步提升样本效率。该框架为可靠性与安全关键决策提供了理论保证下的实用方法。

Abstract: Reliability set estimation identifies input regions where a response probability exceeds a target level, bridging estimation and safety-critical decisions. Practitioners typically start with a working model, an imperfect approximation of the true response surface. Relying on this imperfect model may incur decision risk, potentially certifying unsafe regions as safe. We develop a unified framework that turns such a working model into a trustworthy decision rule. First, a modeling-then-calibration procedure decouples estimation from decision. Since the true set is unobservable, we introduce an asymmetric, observable surrogate loss and use a separate calibration set to select a bias-correcting threshold, reducing decision risk and achieving $O_P(1/n)$ volume convergence. Second, we leverage conformal risk control with the surrogate loss to control false inclusion risk, which is the most safety-critical error, at a pre-specified level regardless of working model quality. Together, these calibration procedures show that a separate calibration set is necessary for risk control. Third, an adaptive design concentrates observations on the reliability set and its boundary, improving model quality where errors most affect decisions while controlling budget elsewhere. Numerical studies show not only more accurate set estimates but also calibrated finite-sample risk control that classical plug-in methods lack.

</details>


### [156] [Combining Concurrent and Historical Functional Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19874)
*Alois Kneip,Dominik Liebl,Sven Otto*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究函数型数据中对函数线性回归模型，其中响应在时刻t同时依赖预测变量的历史轨迹和当前值。模型结合了L²历史效应与点评估效应，证明在协方差算子的特征函数非逐点平方可和条件下，两种效应可分别识别。提出平滑样条估计量并建立收敛速率，揭示了路径光滑性与特征值衰减之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数型线性回归中，响应在时刻t可能同时受预测变量的历史轨迹影响（历史效应）和当前值影响（同步效应），但这两个系数函数在此类模型中天然存在不可识别性问题。厘清两者的可识别条件对于正确建模和估计至关重要，但目前缺乏系统的理论刻画和有效的估计方法。

Method: 首先通过协方差算子特征函数的逐点平方可和性条件刻画非可识别性，证明当特征函数不满足逐点平方可和时，同步效应与历史效应可分别识别。基于预测过程的正交化表示，提出平滑样条估计量用于同时估计两个系数函数，并建立其收敛速率。

Result: 建立了一个温和而新颖的可识别性条件，该条件阻止同步点评估被L²历史效应所表示。估计量的收敛速率揭示了有趣的权衡：预测变量路径越光滑，历史效应估计量收敛越快；路径越粗糙，同步效应估计量收敛越快。模拟研究与两个真实数据应用验证了分离同步与历史效应的实际重要性。

Conclusion: 论文成功解决了函数对函数线性回归中同步效应与历史效应不可识别的问题，提出了理论可识别条件并开发了有效的平滑样条估计方法。路径正则性与特征值衰减之间的权衡为该模型的估计提供了深刻的理论洞见，证明了同时建模并分离两种效应的实用价值。

Abstract: We study a function-on-function linear regression model in which the response at time $t$ depends on both the past trajectory of a predictor and its concurrent value. The model combines an $L^2$-historical effect with a point-evaluation effect, and these two coefficient functions are not automatically identifiable. We characterize the resulting non-identifiability and show that the concurrent and historical effects are separately identifiable whenever the covariance eigenfunctions of the covariance operator of the predictor are not pointwise square-summable. This mild and novel condition prevents the concurrent point evaluation from being represented by an $L^2$-historical effect.
  Building on an orthogonalized representation of the predictor process, we propose a smoothing-spline estimator for both coefficient functions and establish consistency rates. The rates reveal an interesting trade-off between path regularity and eigenvalue decay: smoother predictor trajectories lead to faster convergence of the historical-effect estimator, whereas rougher trajectories lead to faster convergence of the concurrent-effect estimator. Simulation studies and two real-data applications demonstrate the practical importance of disentangling concurrent and historical effects.

</details>


### [157] [The exact Spearman rho-footrule region via optimal transport with applications to finite rankings, mixability, and Chatterjee's rank correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20176)
*Jonathan Ansari,Marcus Rockel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了Spearman's rho在给定Spearman's footrule条件下的最大值这一开放问题，完整刻画了两者的可达区域。作者将底层copula优化问题转化为带线性矩约束的最优传输问题，通过匹配可行的对偶势函数及其接触集构造了唯一最优耦合。该结果有三个应用：改进有限排名中Spearman's footrule距离与二次秩差之间的Cauchy-Schwarz不等式、刻画广义可混性框架下|U'+V'|的可达常数并确定给定均值下的最小二次偏差、推导Chatterjee秩相关ξ(X,Y)与copula相关比之间的显式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spearman's rho和Spearman's footrule是两种最常用的秩相关度量，但两者联合可达区域中给定footrule条件下rho的最大值问题长期未解。解决该开放问题不仅补全了这两个统计量的精确可达区域理论，还能为有限排名分析、广义可混性和现代秩相关度量（如Chatterjee's ξ）提供新的理论工具。

Method: 本文将copula优化问题重新表述为带线性矩约束的经典最优传输问题，具体目标是构造在给定E|U-V|均值条件下最小化Var(|U-V|)的唯一最优耦合。通过构造匹配的可行对偶势函数并确定其接触集（contact set），从对偶角度求解原问题并证明最优解的唯性。这种方法将统计依赖度量问题转化为传输理论中的矩约束优化问题。

Result: 获得了三个主要结果：(1) 在有限排名情境下，得到了Spearman's footrule距离与二次秩差之间改进的Cauchy-Schwarz不等式；(2) 在广义可混性框架下，刻画了U',V'~U(-1/2,1/2)时|U'+V'|的可达常数值，并确定了给定均值条件下最小的二次偏差；(3) 通过利用条件i.i.d.表示，推导出Chatterjee秩相关ξ(X,Y)与copula相关比之间的显式约束界。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解决了存在已久的开放问题（footrule给定下rho的最大值），更展示了最优传输方法在秩相关统计理论中的强大作用。其应用覆盖有限排名比较、广义可混性和现代依赖度量等多个方向，为秩相关度量之间的精确关系提供了系统性的理论框架。

Abstract: We solve the open problem of determining the maximal value of Spearman's rho when Spearman's footrule is prescribed, thereby completing the exact attainable region of these two quantities. To prove this result, we reformulate the underlying copula optimization problem as an optimal transport problem with a linear moment constraint and construct the unique optimal coupling through a matching feasible dual potential and its contact set. Equivalently, this coupling minimizes the variance of $|U-V|$ among all couplings of $U,V\sim\mathcal{U}(0,1)$ with prescribed mean $\mathbb{E}|U-V|$. Our main result admits several applications: First, in the context of finite rankings, we obtain an improved Cauchy--Schwarz inequality between Spearman's footrule distance and the associated quadratic rank difference. Second, in the framework of generalized mixability, we characterize the attainable constant values of $|U'+V'|$, for $U',V'\sim\mathcal{U}(-1/2,1/2)$, and determine the minimal quadratic deviation for a given mean. Third, we derive explicit bounds relating Chatterjee's rank correlation $ξ(X,Y)$, which can detect complex functional dependence of $Y$ on $X$, to the copula correlation ratio--a rank-based fraction of explained variance--by exploiting their conditional i.i.d. representations in terms of Spearman's footrule and Spearman's rho.

</details>


### [158] [Self-Normalizing Denominators in Rational Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20223)
*Shu Tamano*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究线性结构方程模型中理性因果估计量的弱识别问题。作者发现，在高斯采样下，某些分母的采样变异性与其大小完全成比例（标准化分母在每一样本中恒定），因此这些分母无法进入一阶意义上的弱识别区间。嵌套协方差子式的幂次积在所有维度下都具有该性质，并存在精确的Wishart枢轴量。在二维中该结论完备，三维中一个混合族仍未解决，但因子-秩准则可分类所有具有线性或二次无行列式因子的分母，涵盖工具变量、前门和近端公式。对于线性前门调整，即使中介残差方差以任意速率消失，只要处理-中介系数非零，Wald推断仍渐近有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构方程模型中，理性因果估计量是协方差多项式的比值，小分母常被解读为弱识别。但作者发现并非所有分母都能进入弱识别机制，有些分母的采样变异性与其大小严格成比例，导致标准化分母恒为常数。这一发现挑战了传统"小分母即弱识别"的判断，需要系统性识别这类特殊分母结构。

Method: 作者结合高斯采样理论、Wishart分布性质、协方差子式图论以及因子-秩分析。具体包括：(1)证明嵌套协方差子式的幂次积的采样变异性与大小成比例；(2)在二维中给出完整逆定理；(3)在三维中提出因子-秩准则分类线性和二次无行列式因子；(4)针对线性前门调整推导中介残差方差消失时Wald推断的渐近有效性。

Result: 嵌套协方差子式的幂次积在所有维度下具有标准化分母恒定的性质并具有精确Wishart枢轴量；二维中的逆定理完备；三维中分类了大部分情况（一个混合族仍开放）；线性前门公式在中介残差方差任意速率消失时仍保持Wald推断渐近有效；模拟实验证明前门覆盖保持名义水平而近端Wald覆盖随朴素诊断增强而下降。

Conclusion: 并非所有小分母都意味着弱识别；存在一类分母结构（嵌套协方差子式幂积）其识别强度在采样中天然恒定。这一发现对工具变量、前门调整和近端推断等因果推断方法的使用者具有重要实践意义，提示诊断弱识别需区分"天然而强"的分母与真正可能弱识别的分母。

Abstract: Rational causal estimators in linear structural equation models take the form of one covariance polynomial divided by another, and a small denominator is commonly interpreted as weak identification. We show that, under Gaussian sampling, some denominators cannot enter this regime at first order. Their sampling variation is exactly proportional to their magnitude, so the standardized denominator is constant in every sample. Products of powers of nested covariance minors have this property in every dimension and admit an exact Wishart pivot. The converse is complete in dimension two. In dimension three, one mixed family remains open, while a factor-and-rank criterion classifies all denominators with linear or quadratic determinant-free factors and covers instrumental-variable, front-door and proximal formulas. For linear front-door adjustment, Wald inference remains asymptotically valid even as the mediator residual variance vanishes at an arbitrary rate, provided the treatment--mediator coefficient is nonzero. In simulations, proximal Wald coverage fell as a naive treatment--proxy diagnostic strengthened, while front-door coverage stayed nominal, and right-heart-catheterization data distinguished naive from denominator-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [159] [Large Sample Properties of Higher Order Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20321)
*Tuhin Majumder,Donald E. K. Martin,Soumendra N. Lahiri*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限字母表上高阶马尔可夫链的大样本性质，允许阶数m_n随序列长度n增长。通过将过程嵌入Σ^{m_n}上的首阶链并利用返回时间分解，在自然遍历性和稀疏性条件下，为加性泛函建立了中心极限定理。归一化涉及平稳返回时间，适用于m_n→∞且m_n/n→0的三角阵列。作者在二元变长度马尔可夫链（VLMC）中具体化了假设，推导出平稳质量的下界，得到确保CLT成立的显式增长条件（如m_n log m_n / n → 0）。这些结果为稀疏/分区高阶模型（包括VLMC和稀疏马尔可夫模型SMM）的推断提供了渐近基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高阶马尔可夫链的阶数m_n随样本量n增长，经典的固定阶数渐近理论不再适用。该研究旨在为这类高维增长场景下的统计推断建立坚实的渐近理论基础，特别是为稀疏/分区高阶模型（如VLMC和SMM）中的有效维度随样本量增长的情况提供CLT保障。

Method: 将高阶链嵌入Σ^{m_n}上的首阶马尔可夫链，利用返回时间分解（return-time decomposition）来处理状态空间呈指数增长的困难。在自然遍历性和稀疏性条件下，以平稳返回时间到适当选择状态作为归一化因子，建立加性泛函的中心极限定理，并处理m_n→∞且m_n/n→0的三角阵列情形。随后在二元VLMC中推导平稳质量解析下界，将抽象条件转化为可验证的增长速率条件。

Result: 建立了高阶马尔可夫链加性泛函在阶数随样本量增长时的中心极限定理；在二元VLMC情形下，给出了确保CLT成立的显式增长区间，例如m_n log m_n / n → 0。

Conclusion: 该研究为稀疏/分区高阶马尔可夫模型（VLMC和SMM）在有效维度随样本量增长时的渐近推断提供了理论支撑，填补了高维马尔可夫链渐近理论的空白。

Abstract: We study large-sample properties of higher-order Markov chains on a finite alphabet $Σ$ when the order $m_n$ is allowed to grow with the sequence length $n$. By embedding the process into a first-order chain on $Σ^{m_n}$ and exploiting return-time decompositions, we establish a central limit theorem for additive functionals $\sum_{t}\! g_n(Y_t^{(n)})$ under natural ergodicity and sparsity conditions. The normalization involves the stationary return time to a suitably chosen state and accommodates triangular arrays with $m_n\!\to\!\infty$ and $m_n/n\!\to\!0$. We further illustrate the assumptions in a binary variable length Markov chain (VLMC), deriving explicit lower bounds on stationary masses that yield a concrete growth regime (e.g., $m_n\log m_n/n \to 0$) ensuring the CLT. These results provide asymptotic foundations for inference in sparse/partitioned higher-order models; including VLMCs and sparse Markov models (SMMs) where the effective dimensionality grows with the sample size.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，联合建模字典学习不确定性与部署信号表示不确定性。通过跨字典置信对应保留校准兼容字典与部署兼容稀疏表示，投影到物理支撑空间。理论给出最小极大物理分辨率δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N s²)}，并提出自适应有限候选集方法AEB避免过精度报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习后的稀疏追踪在高度相干字典下，即使物理解释不被校准数据支持，仍可能产生精确的原子支撑。不同的校准兼容字典可能对相同支撑赋予不同的物理意义，导致物理推断不可靠。需要联合考虑字典不确定性与表示不确定性，实现分辨率感知的物理支撑推断。

Method: 提出跨字典置信对应(cross-dictionary confidence correspondence)框架，同时保留校准兼容字典和部署兼容稀疏表示，将生存解释投影到物理支撑空间。对局部相干原子类(分离尺度s)，建立物理分辨率的极小极大理论。计算层面提出主动端点括弧(AEB)，一种自适应有限候选库过程，仅评估仍可能影响物理报告的候选，否则安全粗化或放弃。

Result: 理论结果表明，N个校准信号下最小极大物理分辨率满足δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N s²)}，相对分辨率由方向信息尺度Ns⁶支配。部署复制仅在方向变化不能通过调整活动系数吸收时改善物理定位。有限候选库实验（含四区域合成应用）显示点值plug-in选择器在物理上可能过精确，而AEB以更少候选评估避免无依据细化。

Conclusion: AEB方法能有效避免物理过精确问题，在保持可靠性的同时减少计算开销。该工作为字典学习背景下物理支撑推断提供了具有理论保障的方法框架，揭示了物理分辨率与校准样本量、原子相干尺度之间的标度关系。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [161] [Inadvertent Context Leakage in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19857)
*Jaiden Fairoze,Neal Mangaokar,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Sanjam Garg,Saeed Mahloujifar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI模型在上下文窗口包含敏感用户信息（如日历、凭据、健康记录、财务数据）时，模型良性输出中是否泄漏这些秘密信息，并提出一种新型自适应攻击方法，在八种专有模型上实现了近完美的2位数秘密重建和82%的4位数精确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理需要在上下文中持有敏感用户数据，但现有研究仅关注直接提取（模型拒绝）的场景。本文关注的是：即使模型正确拒绝直接提取，上下文中秘密的存在是否会在模型的良性输出中引入隐藏相关性，从而被重建。此外，攻击者是否能主动设计提示放大这种效应。

Method: 提出一种新颖的自适应攻击方法，假设对底层模型只有黑盒访问权限。实验覆盖八种专有模型，通过对照实验测量模型在普通非对抗性请求下输出中秘密的重建率。此外，训练了两个实际攻击工具：(1) 一个分类器用于从日常自然语言输出中推断用户记忆的语义谓词；(2) 一个RL训练的攻击者用于从生产级代理中提取完整社保号。

Result: 2位数上下文秘密几乎完美重建，4位数秘密达到82%精确匹配。更强能力的模型泄漏更多——更强的指令遵循能力放大对上下文秘密的敏感性，表明泄漏是能力增强的副产品而非可修补的漏洞。该泄漏支持两种实际攻击：语义谓词推断和完整社保号提取。

Conclusion: 在上下文窗口中存在敏感信息时，即使模型拒绝直接提取，其良性输出仍会以隐藏方式泄漏这些秘密。这种泄漏与模型能力正相关，是能力增强的副产品而非可修补的bug，对AI代理的隐私安全构成实际威胁。

Abstract: For AI agents to be useful beyond simple chat, they must hold sensitive user context such as calendars, credentials, health records, and financial data. We study whether the mere presence of such secrets in a model's context window introduces hidden correlations into the model's benign outputs, allowing reconstruction even when the model correctly refuses direct extraction. We further study whether an adversary can actively engineer prompts that amplify this effect, using the model as a covert carrier to transmit secrets through seemingly innocuous text. In both cases, this limited leakage is exploited using a novel adaptive attack that assumes black-box access to the underlying model.
  In controlled experiments across eight proprietary models, we find that 2-digit in-context secrets are reconstructed with near-perfect accuracy and 4-digit secrets at 82\% exact match, all from outputs the model produces in response to ordinary, non-adversarial requests. We observe that more capable models leak more: stronger instruction-following amplifies sensitivity to in-context secrets, suggesting leakage is a byproduct of capability as opposed to a patchable bug. We show this leakage enables two practical attacks: (1) a trained classifier that infers semantic predicates about user memories (e.g., health conditions, financial events) from routine natural-language outputs, and (2) an RL-trained adversary that extracts full Social Security Numbers from a production-style agent.

</details>


### [162] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督式机器学习模型的直升机起飞重量估算方法，利用Airbus全球在役机队的海量数据，并按照EASA机器学习应用概念文件和Eurocae ED-324标准实施学习保证流程。研究提出了机器学习需求、模型描述以及长短期记忆（LSTM）递归神经网络的实现，并在遗留航电计算机上验证了需求的满足，证明该模型可部署于机载目标，用于临界功能如机载告警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量的准确估算对飞行安全和临界功能（如机载告警）至关重要，而传统方法可能依赖人工计算或不够精确。同时，航空领域机器学习应用需要严格的学习保证（learning assurance）流程以确保安全性和合规性，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用监督式机器学习方法，使用Airbus全球在役机队的大规模数据集，提出了一套完整的机器学习需求、模型描述及实现方案。具体采用长短期记忆（LSTM）递归神经网络进行直升机起飞重量估算，并遵循EASA概念论文及Eurocae ED-324标准的学习保证流程来验证需求与实现的一致性。

Result: 该LSTM递归神经网络权重估算器在遗留航电计算机上成功实现并通过需求验证，证明该模型能够满足航空级的合规要求，并且适合部署在机载环境中用于临界功能，如实时起飞重量告警。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基于LSTM的机器学习重量估算器可以在满足航空安全标准（EASA与Eurocae ED-324）的前提下部署于机载航电设备，为机器学习在航空临界功能中的应用提供了可行的实现路径和验证方法。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [163] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量方法d_{TR}，将线性重缩放（LRE）直接集成到距离计算中，无需进行前期的归一化步骤即可处理异构属性，并形式化证明了其满足度量性质、有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策常涉及模糊集（尤其是三角模糊数TFN）表示的不精确信息，而许多现有模糊距离度量假设所有值在同一量纲上，当面对不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需要先进行归一化预处理，这一额外步骤增加了复杂性和潜在误差。

Method: 提出三角模糊重缩放距离d_{TR}，将线性重缩放（LRE）直接嵌入距离计算过程中，在比较模糊数时同步完成归一化；形式化证明了其满足度量的四个性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并证明其有界、尺度不变和原点不变；同时引入权重向量用于维度优先级排序。

Result: d_{TR}被证明是一个真正的度量，且具备有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性等优良性质；结合权重向量后，适用于异构模糊数据场景，如综合指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助。

Conclusion: d_{TR}通过将归一化融入距离计算本身，避免了传统方法的预处理步骤，为异构模糊数据提供了更直接、更稳健的距离度量工具，在多个应用领域具有实用价值。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [164] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K，一个大规模多模态动态心电（ECG）数据集，包含22,980个问答对，源自788份临床Holter记录，并创新性地引入信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；基于此构建Holtercare-Bench基准，用于评估MLLMs在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结方面的能力。实验发现主流MLLMs在超长病理序列处理上存在显著性能差距，但微调后可大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医学应用中表现优异，但大多偏向静态图像或短期信号，在动态心电（ECG）这一关键领域，模型因缺乏高质量数据集和基准，难以处理复杂的时间推理和诊断报告生成任务。

Method: (1) 构建Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态ECG数据集，包含22,980个QA对，源自788份临床Holter记录，并设计新颖的信号-视频-文本三模态对齐机制；(2) 提出Holtercare-Bench多模态基准，评估模型在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度的能力；(3) 对主流MLLMs进行零样本评估及微调实验。

Result: 零样本评估揭示主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后获得了实质性提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLMs在电生理学领域的局限性，并为长期医学多模态大语言模型提供了基础性基准，项目已开源。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [165] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了量子-经典混合机器学习方法在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用，通过将特征编码到量子Hilbert空间并使用保真度量子核与SVM、核PCA逻辑回归相结合，发现量子核模型在片段组学数据上表现优于经典SVM基线，在甲基化数据上虽略逊于经典SVM但仍有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT肺癌筛查虽降低死亡率，但受到依从性和管理挑战的限制。血液cfDNA生物标志物为补充手段，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测变得困难，需要更强大的机器学习方法。

Method: 采用量子-经典混合机器学习框架：先进行特征筛选得到20和40维特征子集；使用角度和密集角度特征映射将特征编码到量子Hilbert空间，并结合多种纠缠策略；通过精确态矢量模拟计算保真度量子核，与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，并与原始特征上的经典SVM对比。

Result: 在重复留出验证中，量子核模型在两个数据集上均达到竞争性性能。对片段组学，多个20特征配置相比经典SVM基线的AUC有所提升；对甲基化，经典SVM取得最高AUC，但部分量子模型保持竞争力且在某些情况下提升了特异性。特征数从20增至40未一致改善性能，且常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法有望成为cfDNA肺癌检测的有效途径，系统性评估了编码与纠缠设计对分类的影响，为量子机器学习在液体活检和早期癌症检测领域的应用提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [166] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用数据集来增强大语言模型（LLM）不确定性量化（UQ）的方法。作者构建简单的分类器，以现有的零样本不确定性分数和相似查询的正确性作为特征，来预测LLM回答的正确性，从而在几乎零额外计算开销的情况下持续超越现有的UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化方法（如口头化置信度、多代采样评分等）多为零样本方法，无需标注数据即可生成不确定性分数。然而在实际部署中，必须针对特定数据集评估这些方法的性能。作者认为，既然有数据集可用，就应该充分利用它来提升UQ效果，而现有方法忽略了这一机会。

Method: 作者构建简单分类器来预测LLM回答的正确性。分类器的特征包括：(1) 现有的零样本不确定性分数；(2) 相似查询的正确性（利用数据集中的标注信息）。该方法作为现有UQ分数的增强层，仅增加极少的计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，利用数据集的方法在多个基准上一致性地超越了现有的零样本不确定性量化分数。

Conclusion: 利用部署前可用的数据集来训练简单分类器，是一种廉价且直接的UQ增强方案，适合实际应用中的LLM安全性部署。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [167] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-vector乘积和子集干预等可解释性测量统一为y = Ax + w的线性测量结构，并给出一个实用的流程：从低成本的测量开始、在留出干预上测试、校准简单失配、在残差结构化时扩展测量族。在多个模型（two-HMM、Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI、Qwen-2.5-7B）上验证了该框架的有效性，包括在控制任务中观察者误差与控制误差的关系，以及一阶与高阶测量的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）中各类方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-vector乘积、子集干预）看似相关但缺乏统一的数学框架，它们在不同的访问假设下测量不同量，难以系统比较、组合和校准。本文旨在统一这些方法的共享测量结构，提供一套可操作的程序来经济地恢复内部机制和干预效应。

Method: 将测量统一表述为y = Ax + w：A描述干预，x为目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基失配。据此提出迭代流程：先用成本最低的测量，在留出干预上按目标尺度验证，校准简单失配，若残差仍结构化则扩展测量族（如提升测量或Hessian-vector乘积）。在two-HMM控制任务、Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI和Qwen-2.5-7B上测试不同访问假设下的测量族选择。

Result: 在two-HMM中，控制误差随观察者误差上升，目标改进可能掩盖扰动态移动；前向访问下稀疏聚合测量以更少干预恢复有限效应图；梯度访问下有限探针改进局部归因图；提升测量和Hessian-vector乘积能恢复一阶图遗漏的交互（Tracr表明所需测量族依赖基的选择）；GPT-2-small IOI上Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是三个跨组对中最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上有限校准使加性拒绝响应图足够，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描提供了统一不同可解释性测量的语言和实用程序，揭示了测量族的选择取决于基、访问假设和目标尺度，且"更复杂的测量并不总是必要"——简单加性模型在有限校准下可能已足够。控制任务可作为观察者估计质量的有效验证场景。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [168] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板（template）的神经网络鲁棒性验证加速技术的有效性及其局限性，提出了"联合稳定神经元"（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标来解释模板子sumption率在不同场景下的巨大差异，并进一步提出了FastCert方法——一种自动在神经网络各层间分配模板的技术，能够在不产生加速收益时完全舍弃模板。在大型覆盖设计L0验证任务集上，FastCert相较现有模板复用技术平均获得1.13倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板复用在某些场景下已被证明能通过重用中间层抽象状态来加速不完备的神经网络鲁棒性验证，但模板加速方法在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的稳健性尚不清楚，缺乏系统性研究来阐明其有效性和边界条件。

Method: 1) 对不同场景下模板子sumption率进行系统性实证研究；2) 提出"联合稳定神经元"指标来解释模板加速效果在场景间的差异；3) 基于该指标设计FastCert方法，自动将模板分配到神经网络各层，并在模板预计不会带来加速时完全弃用模板。

Result: FastCert在大型覆盖设计L0验证任务集上，相较于现有的模板复用技术平均实现了1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速技术在不同场景下的效果差异显著，联合稳定神经元指标可有效预测其适用性；FastCert通过自适应模板分配与舍弃策略显著提升了模板复用技术的实际性能。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [169] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCP（Disease Continuum Positioning），一种纵向贝叶斯学习框架，利用纵向扩散张量成像（DTI）连续估计阿尔茨海默病（AD）的疾病严重程度，并通过DCS（Disease Continuum Score）量化个体在疾病连续体中的位置及其不确定性。在ADNI队列上的实验表明DCP优于现有疾病进展方法，DCS能准确刻画疾病严重程度、具备临床相关性、保持纵向演化规律并预测未来疾病转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD本质上是一个连续的生物学过程，但现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于横断面影像上的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续性进展。

Method: DCP将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，通过联合整合纵向观测与弱临床监督进行纵向贝叶斯学习，从中推导出DCS来量化个体在AD疾病连续体上的位置及其不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明DCP持续优于代表性疾病进展方法；综合验证分析显示DCS能够准确表征疾病严重程度、展现强临床相关性、保持纵向疾病演化特征，并能预测未来的疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种基于影像的定量表示，可超越传统诊断标签和临床评分，实现阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [170] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime，一个用于网络安全事件披露后短期异常损失估计的多分辨率框架，结合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据，并引入动态对比学习目标缓解稀疏监督问题。同时构建了SECURE真实数据集，实验证明其在事件条件化金融损失预测任务上优于现有最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全漏洞披露等网络传播的冲击事件会突然扰乱金融时间序列并造成异常损失。现有的时间序列预测模型主要关注趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件。此外，稀疏的高影响事件和背景市场噪声进一步加剧了这一挑战。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率架构，融合三种信息源：长期市场上下文、短期事件前动态和事件元数据。核心组件包括事件融合模块（将时间序列表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关的近期市场模式）和动态对比学习目标（构建事件感知和时间序列感知的正负样本对来缓解稀疏监督问题）。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估计事件后金融损失方面一致优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其具有更强的事件敏感表示、对不完整元数据的更强鲁棒性，以及更可解释的短期市场影响估计。

Conclusion: EventTime证明了将事件元数据与多分辨率时间序列上下文深度融合，并配合对比学习，是解决罕见外部事件冲击下金融损失预测的有效范式。对不完整元数据的鲁棒性和可解释性表明该方法在实际应用中具有实用性。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [171] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeltaMomentum，将方向感知构建到动量更新规则本身。利用线性层梯度天然的关键-值（key-value）结构，用delta规则更新动量缓冲区，每个方向按出现频率决定遗忘速率。作为任意优化器动量缓冲区的即插即用替代，在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW比AdamW节省最多46%步数达到相同验证损失，且额外计算开销仅为门控MLP线性成本的22.2%-25.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器将动量建模为梯度的指数移动平均（EMA），以固定速率遗忘所有方向。但深度网络训练中输入高度各向异性，少数方向被频繁查询而多数方向很少出现。最近的方法通过在缓冲区外增加额外处理来应对这种各向异性，但动量更新规则本身保持不变。作者希望将方向感知直接融入动量更新规则。

Method: 关键观察：线性层的梯度可分解为作为"键"的输入和作为"值"的输出侧误差。利用这一键值结构，DeltaMomentum使用规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲区，使每个方向以与出现频率相关的速率被遗忘。作者证明了它是有效的动量，无需矩阵求逆即可应用输入侧曲率校正，且在固定和漂移最优值下都比EMA更快清除过时方向。它是任意优化器动量缓冲区的即插即用替代，系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW在67M规模下以最多46.39±4.32%更少步数达到AdamW的验证损失，370M下为22.12±0.80%（三个种子）。1B下在Chinchilla最优预算上增益持续存在。同一协议调优的Muon基线在两种语言模型规模上都高于DeltaAdamW。增益在SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny（CIFAR-10）上也成立。额外计算开销为门控MLP线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，无持久内存。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知融入动量更新规则本身，有效应对了深度学习中梯度的各向异性问题。训练时诊断确认了预测机制：更好的梯度追踪和更健康的输入方向。该方法作为优化器动量缓冲区的通用替代品，在多种模型和任务上展现了持续的性能增益。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [172] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用LLM的上下文学习能力进行结构化、提示条件化的评分合成，无需微调或训练神经网络，在24个表格数据集上优于15个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本通常源于跨特征依赖关系的违反而非简单的边际偏离。现有检测器依赖几何或重构信号，而之前的LLM方法主要侧重于用正常样本微调LLM或生成合成异常，未能充分利用LLM对结构化正常状态知识的理解能力。

Method: 将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖关系和蒸馏原型，组织成提示词用于代码生成；生成的评分引擎评估统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上评估，与15个SOTA基线比较，在混合类型和连续型数据设置中均表现出一致的改进。

Conclusion: 该设计消除了LLM微调和神经网络训练的需求，降低了计算成本，使实际表格系统中的异常检测成为可能。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [173] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何的流形视角研究复参数化网络的损失景观，提出在Kähler信息度量下通过自然梯度下降的优化路径，并聚焦于Calabi-Yau信息流形在负曲率条件下的理论保证与失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复参数化网络（如复数权重神经网络）的参数空间天然具有复流形结构，但现有深度学习理论多基于实参数的欧氏或黎曼几何框架。作者希望从信息论与复几何（特别是Kähler几何与Calabi-Yau流形）的角度，为复参数网络的优化景观建立理论基础，并解释负曲率导致的理论保证失效现象。

Method: 采用信息-几何流形方法：通过Wirtinger Hessian在对数似然势上定义Kähler信息度量；利用交叉熵导出自然梯度下降更新规则，以逆度量缩放损失梯度，使下降路径保持在全纯切丛中；特别关注Calabi-Yau信息流形，在不依赖Calabi猜想拓扑条件的非紧致情境下，通过整体势几何定义度量；利用楔积无处消失的全纯形式构造顶外积条件以获得常行列式条件；并分析固定行列式下近似低秩度量导致的blow-up效应。

Result: 研究发现：Calabi-Yau信息度量下常行列式条件成立时，若度量近似低秩（在特征值容差内），则产生blow-up效应；负截面曲率会破坏损失景观的结构，并与负定Ricci曲率存在内在联系；这些几何条件与神经网络在初始化的渐近行为以及消失或负Ricci曲率下的理论保证失效模式直接相关。

Conclusion: 文章从复几何（Kähler与Calabi-Yau流形）视角为复参数化网络的优化提供了理论框架，揭示了负曲率（特别是负截面曲率与负Ricci曲率）如何颠覆损失景观的凸性与可优化性，将几何分析的工具引入深度学习理论，为理解复参数网络的优化失效机制提供了新的分析途径。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [174] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过3,933次实验，系统研究了概念漂移下三种模型重训策略（周期性重训、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发）vs 不重训基线的效果。核心发现是：最关键的设计决策并非重训策略本身，而是模型是否支持增量学习。在支持增量式学习时，所有策略与基线无显著差异；在不支持时，周期性重训显著优于两类反应式策略，反应式仅在周期性漂移下占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但从业者对何时重训缺乏原则性指导。重训代价高昂、预算有限，且模型从训练到部署存在延迟，期间过期模型仍在服务而数据持续漂移。现有文献缺乏在统一系统模型下、将重训预算与训练+部署延迟显式纳入考量的受控实证研究。
